- (h (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c000001][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] "permanent grassland and permanent pasture" (together referred to as "permanent grassland") means land used to grow grasses or other herbaceous forage naturally (self-seeded) or through cultivation (sown) and that has not been included in the crop rotation of the holding for five years or more, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] and, where the relevant authority so decides[^c-a5ebd657e307877d6eae0025ccd3c9eb] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314], that has not been ploughed up for five years or more; it may include other species such as shrubs and/or trees which can be grazed and, where *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] the relevant authority so decides[^c-09316b81e498d50d12057c8551ddec90] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314], other species such as shrubs and/or trees which produce animal feed, provided that the grasses and other herbaceous forage remain predominant. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] The relevant authority[^c-fb11277253ef8323c98ca06805b07292] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] may also decide to consider as permanent grassland: *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
  - ((i)) land which can be grazed and which forms part of established local practices where grasses and other herbaceous forage are traditionally not predominant in grazing areas; and/or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
  - ((ii)) land which can be grazed where grasses and other herbaceous forage are not predominant or are absent in grazing areas; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
- (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] "grasses or other herbaceous forage" means all herbaceous plants traditionally found in natural pastures or normally included in mixtures of seeds for pastures or meadows in the *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] United Kingdom[^c-92061ab4e3f4408e234d5f41896ee0ea] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314], whether or not used for grazing animals; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
- (j (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] "nurseries" means the following areas of young ligneous (woody) plants grown in the open air for subsequent transplantation: *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
  - vine and root-stock nurseries, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
  - fruit tree and berries nurseries, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
  - ornamental nurseries, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
  - commercial nurseries of forest trees excluding those for the holding's own requirements grown within woodland, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
  - nurseries of trees and bushes for planting in gardens, parks, at the roadside and on embankments (e.g. hedgerow plants, rose trees and other ornamental bushes, ornamental conifers), including in all cases their stocks and young seedlings; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
- (k (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] "short rotation coppice" means areas planted with tree species *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] [^c-3a195969b636ebec725b5e985b345287]... *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] [^c-d963481d0da68392439f39329043a7de]... defined by *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] the relevant authority[^c-0894957881306a05c2c2f79f9bcc0f3b] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] that consist of woody, perennial crops, the rootstock or stools of which remain in the ground after harvesting, with new shoots emerging in the following season and with a maximum harvest cycle *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] [^c-d963481d0da68392439f39329043a7de]... determined by the *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] relevant authority[^c-634851ece49305cf3bb57cf8d02d96e6] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
- (l (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] "sale" means the sale or any other definitive transfer of ownership of land or payment entitlements; it does not include the sale of land where land is transferred to public authorities or for use in the public interest and where the transfer is carried out for non-agricultural purposes; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
- (m (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] "lease" means a rental agreement or similar temporary transaction; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
- (n (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] "transfer" means the lease or sale or actual inheritance or anticipated inheritance of land or payment entitlements or any other definitive transfer thereof; it does not cover the reversion of entitlements upon expiry of a lease; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
- (o) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-3f7ca6a287670c3f5b9199c461f5eca9][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] “constituent nation” means England, Wales, Scotland or Northern Ireland, as the case may be; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
- (p) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] “national reserve” means a reserve established by the relevant authority at the level of the constituent nation; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
- (q) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] “regional reserves” means reserves established by the relevant authority at regional level; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
- (r) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] “relevant authority” means— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
  - (i) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] in relation to England, the Secretary of State; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

### Article 4. Definitions and related provisions[^M_E_3b004192-4f6d-4057-9361-33dae6e1fc86]

*Extent: W*

1. For the purposes of this Regulation, the following definitions shall apply:

- (a) "farmer" means a natural or legal person, or a group of natural or legal persons, regardless of the legal status granted to such group and its members [^V_W_eaea20df-6870-4d7a-9515-2e18b7f42e96]..., whose holding is situated within Wales[^c-7d1ef66a986c156db7ab48e17caaf32d], and who exercises an agricultural activity;
- (b) "holding" means all the units used for agricultural activities and managed by a farmer situated within Wales[^c-e2c5c97a02fa143258b219d9605450c5];
- (c) "agricultural activity" means:
  - ((i)) production, rearing or growing of agricultural products, including harvesting, milking, breeding animals, and keeping animals for farming purposes,
  - ((ii)) maintaining an agricultural area in a state which makes it suitable for grazing or cultivation without preparatory action going beyond usual agricultural methods and machineries, based on criteria established by the relevant authority on the basis of the framework set out in Article 4 of Regulation (EU) 639/2014[^V_W_35572fde-98e1-8962-3eb7-0867496a78fd], or
  - ((iii)) carrying out a minimum activity, defined by the relevant authority[^V_W_c337a87a-440a-3872-b133-5c160652da2a], on agricultural areas naturally kept in a state suitable for grazing or cultivation;
- (d) "agricultural products" means the products, with the exception of fishery products, listed in Annex I to the Treaties [^V_W_b013133a-3377-5751-57a8-ea1086420951]...;
- (e) "agricultural area" means any area taken up by arable land, permanent grassland [^c-66d993c34f53b987c09892f21833377a]..., or permanent crops;
- (f) "arable land" means land cultivated for crop production or areas available for crop production but lying fallow, including areas set aside in accordance with Articles 22, 23 and 24 of Regulation (EC) No 1257/1999, with Article 39 of Regulation (EC) No 1698/2005 and with Article 28 of Regulation (EU) No 1305/2013, irrespective of whether or not that land is under greenhouses or under fixed or mobile cover;
- (g) "permanent crops" means non-rotational crops other than permanent grassland [^c-b8b8abce5041520965745a5e849568dd]... that occupies[^c-798890e52fe26071d3457e02e9f4f4b1] the land for five years or more and yield repeated harvests, including nurseries and short rotation coppice;
- (h)[^V_W_c67d2f6a-9205-4cd1-7e30-d35e5da4cda5] “permanent grassland”[^c-484a5a1946f19fc95f3c671ee45dfd35] means land used to grow grasses or other herbaceous forage naturally (self-seeded) or through cultivation (sown) and that has not been included in the crop rotation of the holding for five years or more, and, where the relevant authority so decides[^V_W_bb8175cf-5352-bf73-afae-d887b3f4d0d8], that has not been ploughed up for five years or more; it may include other species such as shrubs and/or trees which can be grazed and, where the relevant authority so decides[^V_W_c380aee6-10e3-4a28-287b-8de7761ca93c], other species such as shrubs and/or trees which produce animal feed, provided that the grasses and other herbaceous forage remain predominant. The relevant authority[^V_W_0ce82323-150e-776c-209c-a43038d9cdb6] may also decide to consider as permanent grassland:
  - ((i)) land which can be grazed and which forms part of established local practices where grasses and other herbaceous forage are traditionally not predominant in grazing areas; and/or
  - ((ii)) land which can be grazed where grasses and other herbaceous forage are not predominant or are absent in grazing areas;
- (i) "grasses or other herbaceous forage" means all herbaceous plants traditionally found in natural pastures or normally included in mixtures of seeds for pastures or meadows in Wales[^c-a8449d037372115b8d5ed07f39acf269], whether or not used for grazing animals;
- (j) "nurseries" means the following areas of young ligneous (woody) plants grown in the open air for subsequent transplantation:
  - vine and root-stock nurseries,
  - fruit tree and berries nurseries,
  - ornamental nurseries,
  - commercial nurseries of forest trees excluding those for the holding's own requirements grown within woodland,
  - nurseries of trees and bushes for planting in gardens, parks, at the roadside and on embankments (e.g. hedgerow plants, rose trees and other ornamental bushes, ornamental conifers), including in all cases their stocks and young seedlings;
- (k) "short rotation coppice" means areas planted with tree species [^V_W_1299c14e-d61b-c6a0-6d0a-70e473c8867f]... [^V_W_7a010747-56d0-59e8-223d-f65f207bef84]... defined by the relevant authority[^V_W_7f322533-fb3d-0923-e59b-02c2e5885a17] that consist of woody, perennial crops, the rootstock or stools of which remain in the ground after harvesting, with new shoots emerging in the following season and with a maximum harvest cycle [^V_W_7a010747-56d0-59e8-223d-f65f207bef84]... determined by the relevant authority[^V_W_fffa5397-71a0-3033-d716-c3e036afb3d7];
- (l) "sale" means the sale or any other definitive transfer of ownership of land or payment entitlements; it does not include the sale of land where land is transferred to public authorities or for use in the public interest and where the transfer is carried out for non-agricultural purposes;
- (m) "lease" means a rental agreement or similar temporary transaction;
- (n) "transfer" means the lease or sale or actual inheritance or anticipated inheritance of land or payment entitlements or any other definitive transfer thereof; it does not cover the reversion of entitlements upon expiry of a lease;
- (o))[^V_W_10e57914-4498-607f-1a0e-e33229467966] “constituent nation” means England, Wales, Scotland or Northern Ireland, as the case may be;
- (p)) “national reserve” means a reserve established by the relevant authority at the level of the constituent nation;
- (q)) “regional reserves” means reserves established by the relevant authority at regional level;
- (r)) “relevant authority” means—
  - (i)) in relation to England, the Secretary of State;
  - (ii)) in relation to Wales, the Welsh Ministers;
  - (iii)) in relation to Scotland, the Scottish Ministers;
  - (iv)) in relation to Northern Ireland, the Department of Agriculture, Environment and Rural Affairs;
- (s))[^c-796813579850941637b3d8929349a66b] “appropriate authority” means the relevant authority for the constituent nation in which the regulations apply;
- (t))[^c-4fe3f7d4db33a0037d643f5079321fa5] “total ceiling” means the amount determined in accordance with Article 5A.

Notwithstanding points (f) and (h) of the first subparagraph,[^V_W_8a44368d-d638-89a2-c7c6-7c62cc5185ec] a relevant authority[^V_W_953cb50a-7161-948b-4e47-5b425fa636c6] which, prior to 1 January 2018, has[^V_W_b4bff75b-4b4c-c670-e69b-b64b1c12afd5] accepted parcels of land lying fallow as arable land may continue to accept them as such after that date. From 1 January 2018 parcels of land lying fallow which have been accepted as arable land under this subparagraph in 2018 shall become permanent grassland in 2023, or thereafter, if the conditions set out in point (h) are met.

2. For the purposes of point (c) of paragraph 1:[^V_W_141f5565-342c-e89d-30ef-51a4f054ab71]

- (a)) the criteria to be met by farmers in order to fulfil the obligation to maintain an agricultural area in a state suitable for grazing or cultivation, as referred to in point (c)(ii) are the criteria set by the relevant authority prior to exit day;
- (b)) where applicable in a constituent nation, the minimum activity to be carried out on agricultural areas naturally kept in a state suitable for grazing or cultivation, as referred to in point (c)(iii) is as defined by the relevant authority prior to exit day.

The relevant authority[^V_W_adcb30b1-2631-7bfc-e116-ec378e032398] may decide that land which can be grazed and which forms part of established local practices where grasses and other herbaceous forage are traditionally not predominant in grazing areas is to be considered to be permanent grassland, as referred to in point (h) of paragraph 1.

The relevant authority may decide that:

- (a) land that has not been ploughed up for five years or more shall be considered permanent grassland as referred to in point (h) of the first subparagraph of paragraph 1, provided that the land is used to grow grasses or other herbaceous forages naturally (self-seeded) or through cultivation (sown) and that it has not been included in the crop rotation of the holding for five years or more;
- (b) permanent grassland may include other species such as shrubs and/or trees which produce animal feed, in areas where grasses and other herbaceous forage are predominant; and/or
- (c) land which can be grazed where grasses and other herbaceous forage are not predominant or are absent in grazing areas be considered permanent grassland as referred to in point (h) of the first subparagraph of paragraph 1.

The relevant authority may decide, on the basis of objective and non-discriminatory criteria, to apply their decision in accordance with points (b) and/or (c) of the third subparagraph of this paragraph to the whole or a part of the constituent nation[^V_W_a4076c78-d7ff-04a5-65c6-9296dba7d954].

[^c-8451aa44d17628cb398eaf536d69b8bd]...

3.[^V_W_20c7d1d5-441e-74f0-fc59-56b69544a53f] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

### Article 4. Definitions and related provisions[^M_E_a56de2a7-46b6-40fb-b42b-c4a60f63a3d0]

*Extent: S*

1. For the purposes of this Regulation, the following definitions shall apply:

- (a) "farmer" means a natural or legal person, or a group of natural or legal persons, regardless of the legal status granted to such group and its members [^V_S_a978abc4-089b-581e-044f-9e0f5c12c0b8]..., whose holding is situated within Scotland[^c-c6a434136bd4fbcce15f6dc9e503aa03], and who exercises an agricultural activity;
- (b) "holding" means all the units used for agricultural activities and managed by a farmer situated within Scotland[^c-8b92327591a7d480a2ae1323278f24e1];
- (c) "agricultural activity" means:
  - ((i)) production, rearing or growing of agricultural products, including harvesting, milking, breeding animals, and keeping animals for farming purposes,
  - ((ii)) maintaining an agricultural area in a state which makes it suitable for grazing or cultivation without preparatory action going beyond usual agricultural methods and machineries, based on criteria established by the relevant authority on the basis of the framework set out in Article 4 of Regulation (EU) 639/2014[^V_S_2783fcc8-51b1-16a0-4bd8-a3b57f16c45f], or
  - ((iii)) carrying out a minimum activity, defined by the relevant authority[^V_S_dc0a1381-508b-fc69-a0ad-660c2e68c89e], on agricultural areas naturally kept in a state suitable for grazing or cultivation;
- (d) "agricultural products" means the products, with the exception of fishery products, listed in Annex I to the Treaties [^V_S_a454c750-25ea-bc80-4451-2efa9930ef78]...;
- (e) "agricultural area" means any area taken up by arable land, permanent grassland and permanent pasture, or permanent crops;
- (f) "arable land" means land cultivated for crop production or areas available for crop production but lying fallow, including areas set aside in accordance with Articles 22, 23 and 24 of Regulation (EC) No 1257/1999, with Article 39 of Regulation (EC) No 1698/2005 and with Article 28 of Regulation (EU) No 1305/2013, irrespective of whether or not that land is under greenhouses or under fixed or mobile cover;
- (g) "permanent crops" means non-rotational crops other than permanent grassland and permanent pasture that occupy the land for five years or more and yield repeated harvests, including nurseries and short rotation coppice;
- (h)[^V_S_c200319a-84f7-98c3-d6b4-0c45a7925d83] "permanent grassland and permanent pasture" (together referred to as "permanent grassland") means land used to grow grasses or other herbaceous forage naturally (self-seeded) or through cultivation (sown) and that has not been included in the crop rotation of the holding for five years or more, and, where the relevant authority so decides[^V_S_5d3313ef-70a4-e110-a16f-1b55a9293382], that has not been ploughed up for five years or more; it may include other species such as shrubs and/or trees which can be grazed and, where the relevant authority so decides[^V_S_c85065b3-7823-9991-08d0-f4de773d2a49], other species such as shrubs and/or trees which produce animal feed, provided that the grasses and other herbaceous forage remain predominant. The relevant authority[^V_S_61f6d60d-da07-2521-e5fd-81450875917e] may also decide to consider as permanent grassland:
  - ((i)) land which can be grazed and which forms part of established local practices where grasses and other herbaceous forage are traditionally not predominant in grazing areas; and/or
  - ((ii)) land which can be grazed where grasses and other herbaceous forage are not predominant or are absent in grazing areas;
- (i) "grasses or other herbaceous forage" means all herbaceous plants traditionally found in natural pastures or normally included in mixtures of seeds for pastures or meadows in the United Kingdom[^V_S_34f6dafc-0fdd-6641-fee5-e153df5ab08d], whether or not used for grazing animals;
- (j) "nurseries" means the following areas of young ligneous (woody) plants grown in the open air for subsequent transplantation:
  - vine and root-stock nurseries,
  - fruit tree and berries nurseries,
  - ornamental nurseries,
  - commercial nurseries of forest trees excluding those for the holding's own requirements grown within woodland,
  - nurseries of trees and bushes for planting in gardens, parks, at the roadside and on embankments (e.g. hedgerow plants, rose trees and other ornamental bushes, ornamental conifers), including in all cases their stocks and young seedlings;
- (k) "short rotation coppice" means areas planted with tree species [^V_S_3eeb3161-a6af-35f7-a454-bef017ae8e7e]... [^V_S_bd88c39e-663f-00a7-a4f5-fc1582306c54]... defined by the relevant authority[^V_S_ed19a5f0-a228-c995-49a2-c09e376bf226] that consist of woody, perennial crops, the rootstock or stools of which remain in the ground after harvesting, with new shoots emerging in the following season and with a maximum harvest cycle [^V_S_bd88c39e-663f-00a7-a4f5-fc1582306c54]... determined by the relevant authority[^V_S_2a7b9549-cea7-dc01-ca0c-a91e05ca8d41];
- (l) "sale" means the sale or any other definitive transfer of ownership of land or payment entitlements; it does not include the sale of land where land is transferred to public authorities or for use in the public interest and where the transfer is carried out for non-agricultural purposes;
- (m) "lease" means a rental agreement or similar temporary transaction;
- (n) "transfer" means the lease or sale or actual inheritance or anticipated inheritance of land or payment entitlements or any other definitive transfer thereof; it does not cover the reversion of entitlements upon expiry of a lease;
- (o))[^V_S_b326ec52-dd4e-30be-54c3-0bf55c980eec] “constituent nation” means England, Wales, Scotland or Northern Ireland, as the case may be;
- (p)) “national reserve” means a reserve established by the relevant authority at the level of the constituent nation;
- (q)) “regional reserves” means reserves established by the relevant authority at regional level;
- (r)) “relevant authority” means—
  - (i)) in relation to England, the Secretary of State;
  - (ii)) in relation to Wales, the Welsh Ministers;
  - (iii)) in relation to Scotland, the Scottish Ministers;
  - (iv)) in relation to Northern Ireland, the Department of Agriculture, Environment and Rural Affairs;
- (s)) “appropriate authority” means—
  - (i)) the relevant authority for the constituent nation in which the regulations apply, or
  - (ii)) the Secretary of State:
    - (aa)) in relation to regulations applying in Scotland, if consent is given by the Scottish Ministers;
    - (bb)) in relation to regulations applying in Northern Ireland, if consent is given by the Department of Agriculture, Environment and Rural Affairs;
- (t))[^c-a556c19066ea105a5c59cce4581efc11] “total ceiling” means the amount determined in accordance with Article 5A.

Notwithstanding points (f) and (h) of the first subparagraph,[^V_S_d428b7ab-c636-2542-537d-0fda3cf4fe8e] a relevant authority[^V_S_ed557a57-7216-c202-9130-4f73910d72b1] which, prior to 1 January 2018, has[^V_S_aeb4ea62-9707-a98c-9873-85de26327c5b] accepted parcels of land lying fallow as arable land may continue to accept them as such after that date. From 1 January 2018 parcels of land lying fallow which have been accepted as arable land under this subparagraph in 2018 shall become permanent grassland in 2023, or thereafter, if the conditions set out in point (h) are met.

2. For the purposes of point (c) of paragraph 1:[^V_S_c80cfd72-94b6-d1d6-60c4-2c7ad82d7e5f]

- (a)) the criteria to be met by farmers in order to fulfil the obligation to maintain an agricultural area in a state suitable for grazing or cultivation, as referred to in point (c)(ii) are the criteria set by the relevant authority prior to exit day;
- (b)) where applicable in a constituent nation, the minimum activity to be carried out on agricultural areas naturally kept in a state suitable for grazing or cultivation, as referred to in point (c)(iii) is as defined by the relevant authority prior to exit day.

The relevant authority[^V_S_78dfe775-2abf-f005-4317-59fbaeb12873] may decide that land which can be grazed and which forms part of established local practices where grasses and other herbaceous forage are traditionally not predominant in grazing areas is to be considered to be permanent grassland, as referred to in point (h) of paragraph 1.

The relevant authority may decide that:

- (a) land that has not been ploughed up for five years or more shall be considered permanent grassland as referred to in point (h) of the first subparagraph of paragraph 1, provided that the land is used to grow grasses or other herbaceous forages naturally (self-seeded) or through cultivation (sown) and that it has not been included in the crop rotation of the holding for five years or more;
- (b) permanent grassland may include other species such as shrubs and/or trees which produce animal feed, in areas where grasses and other herbaceous forage are predominant; and/or
- (c) land which can be grazed where grasses and other herbaceous forage are not predominant or are absent in grazing areas be considered permanent grassland as referred to in point (h) of the first subparagraph of paragraph 1.

The relevant authority may decide, on the basis of objective and non-discriminatory criteria, to apply their decision in accordance with points (b) and/or (c) of the third subparagraph of this paragraph to the whole or a part of the constituent nation[^V_S_48e3c3b7-64f2-6dde-3516-2b7c0c8e59f0].

This paragraph only applies where the decisions to which it refers were made and notified to the Commission by:[^V_S_7ec49b0f-5f05-e2ad-9b95-5943df4dd4dd]

- (a)) 31st January 2015, in the case of a decision taken under the second subparagraph;
- (b)) 31st March 2018, in the case of a decision taken under the third or fourth subparagraph.

3.[^V_S_787a2d56-a637-6b76-e790-1f4ad9a9eeff] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

### CHAPTER 1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note) — Common rules on direct payments (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

#### Article 5 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). General common agricultural policy provisions (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013 and the provisions adopted pursuant thereto shall apply to the schemes provided for in this Regulation. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

#### Article 5A (affected by repeal, see note). The total ceiling for England (affected by repeal, see note)[^M_E_b0a75180-9e94-45b1-b97a-97c30d3fa186][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

#### Article 5A. The total ceiling for Wales[^M_E_7eef00a3-3d6d-440b-d4aa-555b8f90f7ba][^c-8465f76eb0d119d456221f95fbb847cf]

*Extent: W*

1. The Welsh Ministers may[^c-b199926294b76effbb890b0e3bd414da] determine the total ceiling for Wales.

2. The Welsh Ministers may[^c-f2fbd13b0511ec6f82c1a01ce2dae7f4] determine the total ceiling at any time before payments are made[^c-bfb0faca6cdaa8117dbda9ab7e7022cd].

3. The Welsh Ministers may[^c-6a6b37a9f213a32626eb4bbf45a7ed6e] publish the total ceiling as soon as practicable after they have determined it under paragraph 1.

4. The total ceiling may[^c-93593a710229c1da0bc94bdd27b6e963] be distributed among all claimed payment entitlements, including the national reserve or the regional reserves, and ceilings set in accordance with Articles 42 and 51 and the amount allocated under Article 53.

5.[^c-2aeb4e180f9d7ab1de611fc7f49bc34a] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

#### Article 5A. The total ceiling for Scotland[^M_E_cf1432b4-29d6-4b29-f38a-1ee1ae145687][^c-d53b0c9284a5f83c47e77474201f6041]

*Extent: S*

1. In determining the total ceiling for Scotland, the Scottish Ministers must determine an amount equivalent to Scotland’s share of the 2020 national ceiling set out in Annex II.

2. The Scottish Ministers must determine the total ceiling before the start of the relevant financial year.

3. When the total ceiling has been determined, the Scottish Ministers must publish that amount as soon as practicable after that determination has been made.

4. The total ceiling, for any claim year, is to be distributed among all allocated payment entitlements, the national reserve or the regional reserves, and ceilings fixed in accordance with Articles 47, 51 and 53, as they apply in Scotland.

5. Where the Scottish Ministers make a decision under Article 22(2) to increase the basic payment scheme ceiling, the total ceiling for that claim year may be exceeded by the increase decided pursuant to that Article.

6. In this Article—

- (a) “claim year” means a period of 12 months beginning with 1 January,
- (b) “financial year” means a period of 12 months beginning with 1 April.

*Extent: N.I.*

#### Article 9. Active farmer[^M_E_xq7cc8pt-tqzw-8z0z-5wok-664e14196b281]

*Extent: W*

1. No direct payments shall be granted to natural or legal persons, or to groups of natural or legal persons, whose agricultural areas are mainly areas naturally kept in a state suitable for grazing or cultivation and who do not carry out on those areas the minimum activity defined by the relevant authority[^V_W_3a9b9ded-4a0a-d86d-7606-c66ed617480c] in accordance with point (b) of Article 4(2).

2.[^c-32d8e432823c0195cdf11eafc78cce2b] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

3.[^c-32d8e432823c0195cdf11eafc78cce2b] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

3a.[^c-32d8e432823c0195cdf11eafc78cce2b] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

4.[^c-32d8e432823c0195cdf11eafc78cce2b] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

5. In order to guarantee the protection of the rights of farmers, the appropriate authority may make regulations[^V_W_8a0d47d4-d1c5-d80c-5c45-d936b9df034a] laying down:

- (a) criteria for determining the cases where a farmer's agricultural area is to be considered to be mainly an area naturally kept in a state suitable for grazing or cultivation;

#### Article 11. Reduction of payments[^M_E_8dc61d18-155f-4e5d-a85c-a052b6f57d08]

*Extent: W*

1. The relevant authority[^V_W_9b29db63-7925-c906-4067-b4fc360fd2f4] shall reduce the amount of direct payments to be granted to a farmer pursuant to Chapter 1 of Title III for a given calendar year by at least 5 % for the part of the amount exceeding £150,000[^c-cf9c281cafb1c480ed34567b88a4dc67].

2. Before applying paragraph 1, the relevant authority[^V_W_94284598-bf0d-c63e-5a2f-f0768967a168] may subtract the salaries linked to an agricultural activity actually paid and declared by the farmer in the previous calendar year, including taxes and social contributions related to employment, from the amount of direct payments to be granted to a farmer pursuant to Chapter 1 of Title III in a given calendar year. Where no data is available on the salaries actually paid and declared by the farmer in the previous calendar year, the most recent data available shall be used.

3. Where the relevant authority[^V_W_f0daa3ab-af91-1976-e698-5b05c879b5d4] decides to grant a redistributive payment to farmers pursuant to Chapter 2 of Title III and to use more than 5 % [^c-bab4058b492ce53479c2618874d61839]... of the total[^c-3e8645e691a92e1f69fd9b201c147851] ceiling [^V_W_cba0ae41-f56a-c540-1ead-52c307be7fec]... for that purpose, it may decide not to apply this Article.

Where the relevant authority decides to grant a redistributive payment to farmers pursuant to Chapter 2 of Title III and the application of the maximum limits set out in Article 41(4) prevents it from using more than 5 % [^c-bab4058b492ce53479c2618874d61839]... of the total ceiling [^V_W_cba0ae41-f56a-c540-1ead-52c307be7fec]... for that purpose, the relevant authority[^V_W_6400bba2-114d-be1f-fd76-000eba8b88ac] may decide not to apply this Article.

4. No advantage by means of avoiding reductions of the payment shall be granted in favour of farmers in respect of whom it is established that they artificially created, after 18 October 2011, the conditions to avoid the effects of this Article.

5.[^V_W_a562a839-9279-7faf-c3b1-02dcf5639770] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

6.[^V_W_a562a839-9279-7faf-c3b1-02dcf5639770] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

#### Article 11. Reduction of payments[^M_E_1beb2811-b0cd-4906-8cef-4a2a031eb800]

*Extent: S*

1. The relevant authority[^V_S_12ad4398-bd7c-4c84-2068-9b8b190033fc] shall reduce the amount of direct payments to be granted to a farmer pursuant to Chapter 1 of Title III for a given calendar year by at least 5 % for the part of the amount exceeding EUR 150 000.

2. Before applying paragraph 1, the relevant authority[^V_S_943259b4-c5a5-baeb-b7a4-00cca64d3bbd] may subtract the salaries linked to an agricultural activity actually paid and declared by the farmer in the previous calendar year, including taxes and social contributions related to employment, from the amount of direct payments to be granted to a farmer pursuant to Chapter 1 of Title III in a given calendar year. Where no data is available on the salaries actually paid and declared by the farmer in the previous calendar year, the most recent data available shall be used.

3.[^c-957eb4f07951d4342413d775a0767936] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

4. No advantage by means of avoiding reductions of the payment shall be granted in favour of farmers in respect of whom it is established that they artificially created, after 18 October 2011, the conditions to avoid the effects of this Article.

4A.[^c-1bb14050bcfd8b7f5cd686ffea11f990] For each calendar year, the estimated product of the reductions made in accordance with this Article must be made available as support for measures under rural development.

4B. References to “rural development” in this Article and Article 14 include measures which are conducive to—

- (a) the conservation or enhancement of the natural beauty or amenity of the countryside (including its flora and fauna and geological and physiographical features) or of any features of archaeological interest there,
- (b) the promotion of the enjoyment of the countryside by the public,
- (c) starting, or improving the productivity of, an agricultural, horticultural or forestry activity or rural business, or
- (d) starting, promoting or improving rural services.

5.[^V_S_3c4250d2-4884-099f-dd41-37d808a2a843] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

6.[^V_S_3c4250d2-4884-099f-dd41-37d808a2a843] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

3 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] Complementary national direct payments shall be granted in accordance with objective criteria and in such a way as to ensure the equal treatment of farmers and to avoid distortions of the market and of competition. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

Support under the *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] basic payment scheme *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b533e30f92c05a9c019736aba5b1902b] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]farm sustainability payment scheme *(Extent: N.I.)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] shall be available to farmers who: *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

##### Article 22. Basic payment scheme ceiling[^M_E_745de931-f965-4382-f357-ec6992afee77][^V_S_faeceb4d-48fe-c88d-deae-90249b7c5dca]

*Extent: S*

1.[^c-e774d19af780110ec7eea22f1eb6773f] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

2.[^c-95292fd691f8290aa1eac30e41a04eac] The basic payment scheme ceiling for any given year is the amount which remains for the basic payment scheme after deducting, from the total ceiling, the ceilings set under Articles 47, 51 and 53 for that year. The relevant authority may decide to increase the amount of the basic payment scheme ceiling. That increase may not exceed 3% of the total ceiling after deduction of the amount resulting from the application of Article 47(1) for the relevant year.

3. The relevant authority may review the decision under paragraph 2 on an annual basis.

4. The total value of all payment entitlements and the national reserve or regional reserves in Scotland[^c-b406a76749ac38c842eee308d8e06a06] must equal [^c-ffdd9ad7423f4e1499eb38bdaec8d552]... the basic payment scheme ceiling in Scotland[^c-a7be18950a257856c1821fd8ff954c42]. [^c-1c003b671f884508d98829d00620a688]...

5.[^V_S_7f4cbbe2-299e-8a7d-986a-28ff8168220c] If the ceiling calculated[^V_S_d473d3e7-722f-3eaf-3887-801a7b0dffee] pursuant to paragraph 2[^c-380c9110a21ec642518621454c225c18] of this Article is different from that of the previous year as a result of any decision taken by the relevant authority[^V_S_b3479593-b977-6e86-b472-3729ea863ce8] in accordance with paragraph 3 of this Article, [^c-3917b44649f9e17956edc45fec4a6894]...Article 14[^V_S_d3629b8f-c91e-4c05-25ba-4f5301a97cb0], [^c-b61a307b0525415b4594b0863fffa9ae]...[^V_S_ebeb3ea4-73ca-be91-5b86-0250059d0b8a]... the second subparagraph of Article 51(1), or Article 53, the relevant authority shall linearly reduce or increase the value of all payment entitlements in order to ensure compliance with paragraph 4 of this Article.

##### Article 23. Regional allocation of the basic payment scheme ceiling[^M_E_ac8238db-f564-478d-bfd8-097dba71ad86][^c-61276a20e8d7217499176b20df86f200]

*Extent: S*

1. The relevant authority may apply the basic payment scheme at regional level, provided that it took a decision to do so by 1 August 2014.[^V_S_b675468e-ef98-a6b2-4e82-b03cc9135085] In such cases, the relevant authority[^V_S_6e6bb27f-502b-2513-1057-f892a8286529] shall define the regions in accordance with objective and non-discriminatory criteria such as their agronomic and socio-economic characteristics, their regional agricultural potential, or their institutional or administrative structure.

[^V_S_4b4791b1-3fa4-9fd2-15b4-72bae2e26c69]...

2. The relevant authority[^V_S_2f56249b-112c-2bdc-55c3-9762fe36c8ad] shall divide the basic payment scheme ceiling in Scotland referred to in Article 22(2)[^c-ca98aeb434d8ce882bb91ecb4de73114] between the regions in accordance with objective and non-discriminatory criteria.

[^V_S_5179a2c0-4c47-6484-7a6e-f3504780f728]...

3. The relevant authority[^V_S_22f9d5c7-2558-6945-e594-c35cc825d541] may decide that the regional ceilings in Scotland[^c-f0e713981c5b10745599b7f927998e86] shall be subject to annual progressive modifications in accordance with pre-established annual steps and objective and non-discriminatory criteria such as agricultural potential or environmental criteria.

4. To the extent necessary to respect the applicable regional ceilings in Scotland[^c-7ebe246f577e00c5deae878204eec327] determined in accordance with paragraph 2 or 3, the relevant authority[^V_S_f14f5181-4d3d-8904-0ed3-d76b796ed933] shall make a linear reduction or increase in the value of the payment entitlements in each of the relevant regions.

5. The relevant authority[^V_S_670f248c-775c-5925-c346-8ba63347e1a6] may decide to cease the application of the basic payment scheme at regional level from a date to be set by the relevant authority[^V_S_42f429f1-c1c2-6d64-1cbf-c65a462417d3].

6.[^V_S_20844b52-fe08-01e1-a10c-940888a2aa42] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] Payment entitlements shall be allocated to farmers who are entitled to be granted direct payments in accordance with Article 9 of this Regulation provided that: *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] they apply for allocation of payment entitlements under the basic payment scheme by the final date for submission of applications in 2015 to be set in accordance with point (b) of the first subparagraph of Article 78 of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013, except in case of force majeure or exceptional circumstances; and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] they were entitled to receive payments, before any reduction or exclusion provided for in Chapter 4 of Title II of Regulation (EC) No 73/2009, in respect of an aid application for direct payments, for transitional national aid or for complementary national direct payments in accordance with Regulation (EC) No 73/2009 for 2013. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

The first subparagraph shall not apply in Member States applying Article 21(3) of this Regulation. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

Member States may allocate payment entitlements to farmers who are entitled to be granted direct payments in accordance with Article 9 of this Regulation, who fulfil the condition provided for in point (a) of the first subparagraph and who: *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] did not receive payments for 2013 in respect of an aid application as referred to in the first subparagraph of this paragraph and who, on the date fixed by the Member State concerned in accordance with Article 11(2) of Commission Regulation (EC) No 1122/2009 *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314][^f00022] for the claim year 2013: *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
  - ((i)) in Member States applying the single payment scheme: *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
    - were producing fruits, vegetables, ware potatoes, seed potatoes or ornamental plants, and did so on a minimum area expressed in hectares if the Member State concerned decides to adopt such a requirement, or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
    - were cultivating vineyards; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
  - ((ii)) in Member States applying the single area payment scheme, had only agricultural land that was not in good agricultural condition on 30 June 2003 as provided for in Article 124(1) of Regulation (EC) No 73/2009; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] in 2014, are allocated payment entitlements from the national reserve under the single payment scheme pursuant to Article 41 or 57 of Regulation (EC) No 73/2009; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] never held owned or leased-in payment entitlements established under Regulation (EC) No 73/2009 or Regulation (EC) No 1782/2003 and who submit verifiable evidence that, on the date fixed by the Member State in accordance with Article 11(2) of Regulation (EC) No 1122/2009 for the claim year 2013, they produced, reared or grew agricultural products, including through harvesting, milking, breeding animals and keeping animals for farming purposes. Member States may establish their own additional objective and non-discriminatory eligibility criteria for this category of farmers as regards appropriate skills, experience or education. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] Except in the case of force majeure or exceptional circumstances, the number of payment entitlements allocated per farmer in 2015 shall be equal to the number of eligible hectares, which the farmer declares in his aid application in accordance with point (a) of the first subparagraph of Article 72(1) of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013 for 2015 and which are at his disposal on a date fixed by the Member State. That date shall be no later than the date fixed in that Member State for amending such an aid application. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

3 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] Member States may apply one or more of the limitations, as set out in paragraphs 4 to 7, on the number of payment entitlements to be allocated under paragraph 2. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

4 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] Member States may decide that the number of payment entitlements to be allocated shall be equal to either the number of eligible hectares which the farmer declared in accordance with Article 34(2) of Regulation (EC) No 73/2009 in 2013, or the number of eligible hectares referred to in paragraph 2 of this Article, whichever is the lowest. For Croatia, the use of this option shall be without prejudice to the allocation of payment entitlements for de–mined hectares in accordance with Article 20(4) of this Regulation. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

5 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] Where the total number of eligible hectares referred to in paragraph 2 of this Article declared in a Member State would result in an increase of more than 35 % of the total number of eligible hectares declared in accordance with Article 35 of Regulation (EC) No 73/2009 in 2009, or in the case of Croatia in 2013, Member States may limit the number of payment entitlements to be allocated in 2015 to a minimum of either 135 % or 145 % of the total number of eligible hectares declared in 2009, or, in the case of Croatia, of the total number of eligible hectares declared in 2013, in accordance with Article 35 of Regulation (EC) No 73/2009. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

When using this option, Member States shall allocate a reduced number of payment entitlements to farmers. That number shall be calculated by applying a proportional reduction to the additional number of eligible hectares declared by each farmer in 2015 compared to the number of eligible hectares within the meaning of Article 34(2) of Regulation (EC) No 73/2009 that that farmer declared in his aid application in 2011 or, in the case of Croatia, in 2013, without prejudice to the de-mined hectares for which payment entitlements are to be allocated in accordance with Article 20(4) of this Regulation. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

6 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] Member States may decide to apply, for the purposes of establishing the number of payment entitlements to be allocated to a farmer, a reduction coefficient to those eligible hectares referred to in paragraph 2 which consist of permanent grassland located in areas with difficult climate conditions, especially due to their altitude and other natural constraints such as poor soil quality, steepness and water supply. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

7 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] Member States may decide that the number of payment entitlements to be allocated to a farmer shall be equal to the number of eligible hectares referred to in paragraph 2 of this Article which were not hectares of vineyards on the date fixed by the Member State in accordance with Article 11(2) of Regulation (EC) No 1122/2009 for the claim year 2013 or hectares of arable land under permanent greenhouses. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

8 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] In the case of the sale or lease of their holding or part of it, natural or legal persons complying with paragraph 1 of this Article may, by contract signed before the final date for submitting applications in 2015 to be set in accordance with point (b) of the first subparagraph of Article 78 of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013, transfer the right to receive payment entitlements in accordance with paragraph 1 of this Article to one or more farmers provided that the latter comply with the conditions laid down in Article 9 of this Regulation. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

9 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] A Member State may decide to fix a minimum size per holding, expressed in eligible hectares, in respect of which the farmer may apply for an allocation of payment entitlements. That minimum size shall not exceed the threshold set out in point (b) of Article 10(1) in conjunction with paragraph 2 of that Article. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

10 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] Member States shall, where relevant, notify the Commission of the decisions referred to in this Article by 1 August 2014. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

11 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] The Commission shall adopt implementing acts laying down rules on applications for the allocation of payment entitlements submitted in the year of allocation of payment entitlements where those payment entitlements may not yet be definitively established and where that allocation is affected by specific circumstances. Those implementing acts shall be adopted in accordance with the examination procedure referred to in Article 71(2). *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

##### Article 25 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Value of payment entitlements and convergence (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^M_E_de91926b-38c1-492e-c99a-07f7419c6565][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

*Extent: E+N.I.*

1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-c4f9c0367b07962feb23351d4df73967][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] Subject to paragraphs 2 and 3, for the purposes of this Article the unit value of payment entitlements is calculated by dividing a fixed percentage of the *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] total ceiling[^c-ce13e7f949cf2d1116e2635da2040f17] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] for each relevant year by the number of payment entitlements in 2015 in the constituent nation or, where applicable, at regional level, excluding those allocated from the national reserve or regional reserves in 2015. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

The fixed percentage referred to in the first subparagraph is calculated by dividing *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] [^c-6424216cce065aad85ee48e469c94fdc]... the *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] farm sustainability payment scheme[^c-31bb7069e658c80e0ed0ccff6bf1d256] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] ceiling or, where applicable, the regional ceiling for the *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] farm sustainability payment scheme *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] set in accordance with Article 23(2), as it had effect immediately before exit day for 2015, after applying the linear reduction provided for in paragraph 1 or, where applicable, paragraph 2 of Article 30 *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] , as it had effect immediately before exit day,[^c-7d0b89d0e9a4d87c5026cc0be4ddebd1] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] by the relevant authority’s share of the national ceiling for 2015. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] The value of payment entitlements, other than those allocated from the national reserve or regional reserves in 2015, may be differentiated for each relevant year on the basis of their initial unit value calculated in accordance with Article 26 as it had effect immediately before exit day, provided that the decision to differentiate was taken and notified to the Commission by 1 August 2014. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

3 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] All payment entitlements in a constituent nation or, where Article 23 is applied, in a region shall have a uniform unit value unless the relevant authority has applied the derogation in paragraph 4 of this Article, as it had effect immediately before exit day. Where a relevant authority has applied this derogation, the unit value will be calculated in accordance with paragraphs 4 to 7 of this Article as it had effect immediately before exit day. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

8 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] When applying paragraph 2 of this Article, the transition from the initial unit value of payment entitlements as calculated in accordance with Article 26 *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] as it had effect immediately before exit day[^c-32394f34c60563ec7b853fd8451339cf] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] to their final unit value in 2019 as established in accordance with paragraph 3 or paragraphs 4 to 7 of this Article shall be made in equal steps starting from 2015. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

In order to ensure compliance with the fixed percentage referred to in paragraph 1 of this Article for each year, the value of the payment entitlements with an initial unit value that is higher than the national or regional unit value in 2019 shall be adjusted. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

9 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-7c412b02afa2e844c9fc81fecceee492][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

10 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] In 2015, Member States shall inform farmers of the value of their payment entitlements as calculated in accordance with this Article and Articles 26 and 27 for each year of the period covered by this Regulation. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

#### Section 2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note) — National reserve and regional reserves (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

##### Article 30 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Establishment and use of the national reserve or regional reserves (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^M_E_c861a1bb-9388-47f9-c0e4-8a2d0c6fa4a0][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

*Extent: E+N.I.*

1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-6a490d834129229aa3e3e115e90914f7][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] The relevant authority shall maintain the national reserve or regional reserves established prior to exit day. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-507d16a2f07d4539bb29ff3930a7f7da][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

3 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-507d16a2f07d4539bb29ff3930a7f7da][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

4 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] The relevant authority[^c-7af5f609fecd872c637887cbcdc6aa21] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] shall allocate payment entitlements from their national or regional reserves in accordance with objective criteria and in such a way as to ensure the equal treatment of farmers and to avoid distortions of the market and of competition. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

5 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-d276b197bf44b0269866b0c122c0fbdd][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

6 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] The relevant authority[^c-7e237975ec5ed8a90e98c799de9b0552] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] shall use their national or regional reserves to allocate payment entitlements, as a matter of priority, to young farmers and to farmers commencing their agricultural activity. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

7 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] The relevant authority[^c-d76b6c107499f45051ddf31967656c63] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] may use their national or regional reserves to: *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] allocate payment entitlements to farmers in order to prevent land from being abandoned, including in areas subject to restructuring or development programmes relating to a form of public intervention; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] allocate payment entitlements to farmers in order to compensate them for specific disadvantages; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] allocate payment entitlements to farmers who were prevented from being allocated payment entitlements under this Chapter as a result of force majeure or exceptional circumstances; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

The relevant authority[^c-bfb70b1626bbfd79b67062981b94c4eb] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] shall fix the steps for annual progressive modifications of the value of payment entitlements allocated from the national reserve or regional reserves, taking account of the modifications of the *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] relevant authority’s share of the basic payment ceiling or, where applicable, the regional ceiling[^c-be187d2a088907aeeddd03b264d5617a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] in Northern Ireland *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] for the *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] farm sustainability payment scheme *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] set in accordance with *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] Article 23(2) that result from the variations in the level of the national ceilings set out in Annex II. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

However, that date shall not be later than the latest date for lodging an application under the *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] farm sustainability payment scheme *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] following the date of the court ruling or the administrative act, taking into account the application of Articles 32 and 33. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

10a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-820da59eb893e72dd4a6bad50a932477][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] When applying paragraph 6, points (a) and (b) of paragraph 7, and paragraph 10, the sum of the number of payment entitlements allocated to a farmer and the number of existing entitlements held by that farmer where their unit value is increased to the national or regional average value shall not exceed 90. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

(10b. (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-9a01f2d3488ca49b28d75a82bdd4a828][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] After the application of paragraph (10a), the number of payment entitlements to be allocated to a farmer shall be reduced by the number of entitlements temporarily transferred out by that farmer and by the number of entitlements permanently transferred out by that farmer in the scheme year of application and the previous 2 scheme years, provided those scheme years relate to 2022 or later. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

11 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] For the purposes of this Article, the following definitions shall apply: *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] 'young farmers' means farmers fulfilling the conditions laid down in Article 50(2) and, where relevant, the conditions referred to in Article 50(3) and (11); *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] 'farmers commencing their agricultural activity' means natural or legal persons who, in the five years preceding the start of the agricultural activity, did not have any agricultural activity in their own name and at their own risk or did not have the control of a legal person exercising an agricultural activity. In the case of a legal person, the natural person or persons in control of the legal person must not have had any agricultural activity in their own name and at their own risk or must not have had the control of a legal person exercising an agricultural activity in the five years preceding the start of the agricultural activity by the legal person; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] this category of farmers must also meet any additional objective and non-discriminatory eligibility criteria established by the relevant authority prior to exit day as regards appropriate skills, experience or education[^c-4669f7b26a2aa767c439c5f163c44829] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

(12. (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-b9c762ae0cd6475b4295fb73fa07ea2a][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] A farmer as defined in Article 4(1)(a) who has submitted an application and been unsuccessful in obtaining entitlements from the national or regional reserve under paragraph 6, 7a or 7b in three or more scheme years shall no longer be eligible to be allocated entitlements from the national or regional reserve under paragraph 6, 7a or 7b. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

A young farmer as defined in Article 50(2) who has submitted an application and has been unsuccessful in obtaining entitlements from the national or regional reserve under paragraph 6, 7a or 7b in three or more scheme years shall no longer be eligible to be allocated entitlements from the national or regional reserve under paragraph 6, 7a or 7b. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

13 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-8c4cca6eed91dcf050f96345b6708317][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] A young farmer or a farmer commencing their agricultural activity is not eligible to apply for an allocation of payment entitlements in a year after 2025. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

##### Article 30. Establishment and use of the national reserve or regional reserves[^M_E_c26a80a2-d9e0-42f0-ce3e-310e25eb9aaa][^c-7a3a7418fce0a1a7b892ee25f148d9e1]

*Extent: W*

. . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

##### Article 30. Establishment and use of the national reserve or regional reserves[^M_E_e28411fd-4830-4f32-c3cf-783c77b0592f]

*Extent: S*

1.[^V_S_a0f7b0c7-3d85-c834-6808-c541097e68ce] The relevant authority shall maintain the national reserve or regional reserves established prior to exit day.

2.[^V_S_fb182c3b-c19b-e982-5090-6932651a2ffc] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

3.[^V_S_fb182c3b-c19b-e982-5090-6932651a2ffc] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

4. The relevant authority[^V_S_c8f90c79-0abd-5a43-3db5-688bd733b3b5] shall allocate payment entitlements from their national or regional reserves in accordance with objective criteria and in such a way as to ensure the equal treatment of farmers and to avoid distortions of the market and of competition.

5. Payment entitlements referred to in paragraph 4 shall only be allocated to farmers entitled to be granted direct payments in accordance with Article 9.

6. The relevant authority[^V_S_201fcf92-f243-8a23-2db3-f8901138d6b2] shall use their national or regional reserves to allocate payment entitlements, as a matter of priority, to young farmers and to farmers commencing their agricultural activity.

7. The relevant authority[^V_S_b0002917-8c38-fb74-b67d-a52a520ce199] may use their national or regional reserves to:

- (a) allocate payment entitlements to farmers in order to prevent land from being abandoned, including in areas subject to restructuring or development programmes relating to a form of public intervention;
- (b) allocate payment entitlements to farmers in order to compensate them for specific disadvantages;
- (c) allocate payment entitlements to farmers who were prevented from being allocated payment entitlements under this Chapter as a result of force majeure or exceptional circumstances;
- (d) allocate, in cases where they apply Article 21(3) of this Regulation, payment entitlements to farmers whose number of eligible hectares that they declared in 2015 in accordance with point (a) of the first subparagraph of Article 72(1) of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013 and that are at their disposal on a date fixed by the Member State, which shall be no later than the date fixed in that Member State for amending such an aid application, is higher than the number of owned or leased-in payment entitlements established in accordance with Regulation (EC) No 1782/2003 and with Regulation (EC) No 73/2009 that they hold on the final date for submission of applications to be set in accordance with point (b) of the first subparagraph of Article 78 of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013;
- (e) linearly increase, on a permanent basis, the value of all payment entitlements under the basic payment scheme at national or regional level if the relevant national or regional reserve exceeds 0,5 % of the basic payment scheme ceiling in Scotland[^c-086acce316f7913ffbefa2e2c73223a6][^V_S_e58c239d-f566-d663-1f99-c04f770c4420] or, where applicable, the regional ceiling in Scotland[^c-85cb902798afbc64c32deaf39a4235c9] for the basic payment scheme, provided that sufficient amounts remain available for allocations under paragraph 6, under points (a) and (b) of this paragraph and under paragraph 9 of this Article;
- (f) cover the yearly needs for payments to be granted in accordance with Article 51(2) [^c-afb5832b2765e12915aa4568e5cee236]... of this Regulation.

For the purpose of this paragraph, the relevant authority[^V_S_385d8149-af6e-8eef-aaaa-ddf5db32232a] shall decide on the priorities between the different uses referred to herein.

8. When applying paragraph 6 and points (a) and (b)[^V_S_3f86883f-9258-c2fc-6432-55257c13849f] of paragraph 7, the relevant authority[^V_S_f781da42-27e0-e123-174c-60471b52a316] shall fix the value of payment entitlements allocated to farmers at the national or regional average value of payment entitlements in the year of allocation.

The national or regional average value shall be calculated by dividing the[^V_S_6b92560d-418f-4527-ff2d-8b3b74bd63a3] basic payment scheme ceiling in Scotland or, where applicable, the regional ceiling in Scotland for the basic payment scheme set in accordance with Article 23(2), for the year of allocation, excluding the amount of the national reserve or regional reserves, by the number of allocated payment entitlements.

[^c-ca284bbacb2a4bd992594500025f31fb]...

9. Where a farmer is entitled to receive payment entitlements or to increase the value of the existing ones by virtue of a definitive court ruling or by virtue of a definitive administrative act of the competent authority of a relevant authority[^V_S_c8ef9514-0f81-e625-cbd8-24ac05bc9254], the farmer shall receive the number and value of payment entitlements established in that ruling or act at a date to be fixed by the relevant authority. However, that date shall not be later than the latest date for lodging an application under the basic payment scheme following the date of the court ruling or the administrative act, taking into account the application of Articles 32 and 33.

10. When applying paragraph 6, points (a) and (b) of paragraph 7 and paragraph 9, the relevant authority[^V_S_3538057e-bde0-dfa8-ee06-b0fa8de32411] may either allocate new entitlements or increase the unit value of all of the existing entitlements of a farmer up to the national or regional average value.

11. For the purposes of this Article, the following definitions shall apply:

- (a) 'young farmers' means farmers fulfilling the conditions laid down in Article 50(2) and, where relevant, the conditions referred to in Article 50(3) and (11);
- (b) 'farmers commencing their agricultural activity' means natural or legal persons who, in the five years preceding the start of the agricultural activity, did not have any agricultural activity in their own name and at their own risk or did not have the control of a legal person exercising an agricultural activity. In the case of a legal person, the natural person or persons in control of the legal person must not have had any agricultural activity in their own name and at their own risk or must not have had the control of a legal person exercising an agricultural activity in the five years preceding the start of the agricultural activity by the legal person; this category of farmers must also meet any additional objective and non-discriminatory eligibility criteria established by the relevant authority prior to exit day as regards appropriate skills, experience or education[^V_S_e0a33307-b7a4-4668-a0d5-6d26e1278906].

- (iii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] the Farm Sustainability *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] Payment[^c-c57cbc4e950788e1d76ceb6a7dbda551] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] Scheme *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] For the purposes of this Title, 'eligible hectare' means: *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] any agricultural area of the holding *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] [^c-82f1b435eae691240f0a77867a26ac9e]... that is used for an agricultural activity or, where the area is also used for non-agricultural activities, is predominantly used for agricultural activities; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

*Extent: N.I.*

- (aa (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-464677b9f0bbda5f196e4ee807eba80e][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] any area of the holding comprising– *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] blanket bog or lowland raised bog; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] a discrete area of woodland less than 5 hectares; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
  - (iii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] such vegetative features as the relevant authority may determine; or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
  - (iv (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] up to and including seventy per cent stones, scree rock or scattered rock; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
- (b (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-af71945f199974b2f002a4158cb15a85][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] any area which gave a right to payments in 2008 under the single payment scheme laid down in Title III[^c-08eece6f8ba9c5d79a7a2203705ae4d4] *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-af71945f199974b2f002a4158cb15a85] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] of Regulation (EC) No 1782/2003, and which: *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-af71945f199974b2f002a4158cb15a85] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
  - ((i)) no longer complies with the definition of 'eligible hectare' under point (a) as a result of the implementation of Directive 92/43/EEC, Directive 2000/60/EC and Directive 2009/147/EC; *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-af71945f199974b2f002a4158cb15a85] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
  - ((ii)) for the duration of the relevant commitment by the individual farmer, is afforested pursuant to Article 31 of Regulation (EC) No 1257/1999 or to Article 43 of Regulation (EC) No 1698/2005 or to Article 22 of Regulation (EU) No 1305/2013 or under a national scheme the conditions of which comply with Article 43(1), (2) and (3) of Regulation (EC) No 1698/2005 or Article 22 of Regulation (EU) No 1305/2013; or *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-af71945f199974b2f002a4158cb15a85] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
