3 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] For the purposes of point (a) of paragraph 2: *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] where an agricultural area of a holding is also used for non-agricultural activities, that area shall be considered to be used predominantly for agricultural activities provided that those agricultural activities can be exercised without being significantly hampered by the intensity, nature, duration and timing of the non-agricultural activities; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] the relevant authority[^c-d89b954e8305296c4cee08d97ded7793] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] may draw up a list of areas which are predominantly used for non-agricultural activities. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

The relevant authority must apply the criteria it has established for the implementation of this paragraph in the constituent nation.[^c-c2fae76863b21c4b86fc0d5e7a16cab1] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

4 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] Areas shall be considered to be eligible hectares only if they comply with the definition of eligible hectare throughout the calendar year, except in the case of force majeure or exceptional circumstances. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

5 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-64e76714e6959b958577e341dbcf0ca0][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] For the purposes of determining 'eligible hectare', *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-64e76714e6959b958577e341dbcf0ca0] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] the relevant authority[^c-8e66c44cff739db28bb146411c8e80b5] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-64e76714e6959b958577e341dbcf0ca0] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] having taken the decision referred to in the second subparagraph of Article 4(2) may apply a reduction coefficient to convert the hectares concerned into 'eligible hectares'. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-64e76714e6959b958577e341dbcf0ca0] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

6 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] Areas used for the production of hemp shall only be eligible hectares if the varieties used have a tetrahydrocannabinol content not exceeding 0,2 %. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

##### Article 32A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-fbd10bcc55a08d2afefee4103dc42dc1][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

*Extent: N.I.*

(1. (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] Farmers shall not convert or plough permanent grassland situated in areas which, prior to exit day, have been designated by the relevant authority as permanent grasslands which are environmentally sensitive. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

(2. (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] Without prejudice to Directive 2004/35/CE of the European Parliament and of the Council where a farmer has converted or ploughed permanent grassland that is subject to the obligation referred to in paragraph 1 the relevant authority shall provide for the obligation to reconvert the area into permanent grassland and may, on a case by case basis, issue precise instructions to be respected by the farmer concerned on how to reverse the environmental damage caused in order to restore the environmentally sensitive status. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

(3. (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] The farmer shall be informed without delay after the non-compliance has been established of the obligation to reconvert and of the date before which that obligation is to be complied with. That date shall not be later than the date for the submission of the single application for the following year. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

(4. (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] By way of derogation from Article 4(1)(h) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013, the land reconverted shall be considered as permanent grassland as of the first day of reconversion and be subject to the obligation referred to in paragraph 1. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

##### Article 33 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Declaration of eligible hectares (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] For the purposes of the activation of payment entitlements provided for in Article 32(1), the farmer shall declare the parcels corresponding to the eligible hectares accompanying any payment entitlement. #### Article 41. General rules[^M_E_iin9w4vr-8u60-0p7s-bqtf-664f12e91f16c]

*Extent: W*

1.[^V_W_fbc73833-3a91-1395-838e-c7acd81a9bd6] The relevant authority may grant an annual payment to farmers who are entitled to a payment under the basic payment scheme referred to in Sections 1, 2, 3 and 5 of Chapter 1 (“the redistributive payment”) [^c-1364196c873732003f5bbcdaaea1ea7d]....

2. If the relevant authority applies the basic payment scheme at regional level in accordance with Article 23, it may apply the redistributive payment at regional level.

3. Without prejudice to the application [^c-dc576718510ec2d6b680b1db76dbda10]... of reduction of payments in accordance with Article 11 [^c-ab661e4429b366794725f3aa36d585e7]... of this Regulation, and to the application of Article 63 of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013, the redistributive payment shall be granted annually upon activation of payment entitlements by the farmer [^V_W_5ec3060b-feae-43c7-733d-2154c5de24b9]....

4. The redistributive payment shall be calculated each year by the relevant authority[^V_W_cde673bc-346d-0ded-def4-57aef280b98f] by multiplying a figure to be set by the relevant authority[^V_W_a95de480-b9f0-31f9-4148-65845da7fdd6], which shall not be higher than 65 % of the [^V_W_a51d137a-e2f6-cc21-9d76-2eed64ab439d]... average payment per hectare made by the relevant authority (at regional level where applicable)[^V_W_e661261f-c8dc-4ab5-3794-8312421c4e50], by the number of payment entitlements activated by the farmer in accordance with Article 33(1) [^V_W_497e494a-af64-c690-3c94-4a8f4de2ef5f].... The number of such payment entitlements or hectares shall not exceed a maximum level to be set by the relevant authority which shall not be higher than 54 hectares.[^c-36e16ef27434b0bdfeff1778d082367f]

5.[^V_W_b5eeba3d-8a30-ab9b-8a49-04218069effd] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

6. The national average payment per hectare referred to in paragraph 4 of this Article shall be established by the relevant authority[^V_W_430abb4d-84e6-3536-ab94-d6b2da3f7951] on the basis of its share[^V_W_0f5b250b-b018-2cc0-9451-ebbf49969a8c] of the national ceiling [^V_W_8ce648a9-01a5-71b0-1c89-eb1df46c422d]... for calendar year 2019 and the number of eligible hectares declared in accordance with Article 33(1) [^V_W_f2f2ae2a-571d-91e4-1a45-408cf76181ef]... in 2015.

[^V_W_709f9c70-1b1d-d9fc-8bf6-773a5f089bd3]...

7. The relevant authority[^V_W_eed163c0-65bf-4440-2b91-2ed6ebd83920] shall ensure that no advantage provided for under this Chapter is granted to farmers in respect of whom it is established that, after 18 October 2011, they divided their holding with the sole purpose of benefiting from the redistributive payment. This shall also apply to farmers whose holdings result from that division.

8.[^V_W_22693e2d-53a9-ad9c-d6df-1d04bceb2a26] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

General rules (affected by repeal, see note) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314][^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] Farmers entitled to a payment under the basic payment scheme or the single area payment scheme shall observe, on all their eligible hectares within the meaning of Article 32(2) to (5), the agricultural practices beneficial for the climate and the environment referred to in paragraph 2 of this Article or the equivalent practices referred to in paragraph 3 of this Article. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-5e302c97e4ab4312cc884abdb231d494][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] The agricultural practices beneficial for the climate and the environment shall be the following: *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note) (affected by repeal, see note) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-fc3166144e7e5b99a5035b3e83b6408c][^c-60901f30418035bb9169094a86ba20d7][^c-497b8367d85d61684979c76bf577f824][^c-26dc191e9fecacda1869b59ac9f9d10c][^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] crop diversification; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-26dc191e9fecacda1869b59ac9f9d10c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] maintaining existing permanent grassland; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] having ecological focus area on the agricultural area. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

3 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] The equivalent practices shall be those which include similar practices that yield an equivalent or higher level of benefit for the climate and the environment compared to one or several of the practices referred to in paragraph 2. Those equivalent practices and the practice or practices referred to in paragraph 2 to which they are equivalent are listed in Annex IX and shall be covered by any of the following: *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] commitments undertaken in accordance with either Article 39(2) of Regulation (EC) No 1698/2005 or Article 28(2) of Regulation (EU) No 1305/2013; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] national or regional environmental certification schemes, including those for the certification of compliance with national environmental legislation, going beyond the relevant mandatory standards established pursuant to Chapter I of Title VI of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013, which aim to meet objectives relating to soil and water quality, biodiversity, landscape preservation, and climate change mitigation and adaptation. Those certification schemes may include the practices listed in Annex IX to this Regulation, the practices referred to in paragraph 2 of this Article, or a combination of those practices. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

4 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] The equivalent practices referred to in paragraph 3 shall not be the subject of double funding. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

5 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] Nothing in this Regulation prevents the relevant authority from deciding[^c-af36d421f3fc5e7a9683420bceb83b4c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314], including, where appropriate, at regional level, to restrict the choice of the farmers to use the options referred to in points (a) and (b) of paragraph 3. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

This payment shall take the form of an annual payment per eligible hectare declared in accordance with Article 33(1) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] [^c-56b9b7a922b2d72eac4ae90b538c1a66]..., the amount of which shall be calculated annually by dividing the amount resulting from the application of Article 47 by the total number of eligible hectares declared in accordance with Article 33(1) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] [^c-56b9b7a922b2d72eac4ae90b538c1a66]... in the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] constituent nation[^c-8234d869fbcfb11888e93ac19c1ca827] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] or the region concerned. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

By way of derogation from the second subparagraph, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] and provided that the decision to derogate was taken and notified to the Commission prior to 15 December 2014, the relevant authority[^c-520492aadab6f0bb0f94029051d4b639] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] may decide to grant the payment referred to in this paragraph as a percentage of the total value of the payment entitlements that the farmer has activated in accordance with Article 33(1) for each relevant year. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

For each year and each *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] constituent nation *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] or region, that percentage shall be calculated by dividing the amount resulting from the application of Article 47 by the total value of all payment entitlements activated in accordance with Article 33(1) in that *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] constituent nation *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] or region. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

10 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] Farmers whose holdings are fully or partly situated in areas covered by Directives 92/43/EEC, 2000/60/EC, or 2009/147/EC shall be entitled to the payment referred to in this Chapter provided that they observe the practices referred to in this Chapter to the extent that those practices are compatible in the holding concerned with the objectives of those Directives. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

11 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] Farmers complying with the requirements laid down in Article 29(1) of Regulation (EC) No 834/2007 as regards organic farming shall be entitled ipso facto to the payment referred to in this Chapter. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

The first subparagraph shall apply only to the units of a holding that are used for organic production in accordance with Article 11 of Regulation (EC) No 834/2007. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

#### Article 44 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note) (affected by repeal, see note) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Crop diversification (affected by repeal, see note) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-bcb99ecd547fd38d039595e580656a8b][^c-1ce5944aea0939efea5222cfc1bd77a1][^c-00ffe28cf77784053b0cd6785f2c8e75][^c-2d7e9bb569e98678bd45470c283e1b10][^c-d6ab8ea77807f3eae612af489ba63707][^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314][^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note) (affected by repeal, see note) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-d6ab8ea77807f3eae612af489ba63707][^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] Where the arable land of the farmer covers between 10 and 30 hectares and is not entirely cultivated with crops under water for a significant part of the year or for a significant part of the crop cycle, there shall be at least two different crops on that arable land. The main crop shall not cover more than 75 % of that arable land. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d6ab8ea77807f3eae612af489ba63707] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

Where the arable land of the farmer covers more than 30 hectares and is not entirely cultivated with crops under water for a significant part of the year or for a significant part of the crop cycle, there shall be at least three different crops on that arable land. The main crop shall not cover more than 75 % of that arable land and the two main crops together shall not cover more than 95 % of that arable land. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d6ab8ea77807f3eae612af489ba63707] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note) (affected by repeal, see note) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-d6ab8ea77807f3eae612af489ba63707][^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] Without prejudice to the number of crops required pursuant to paragraph 1, the maximum thresholds set out therein shall not apply to holdings where grasses or other herbaceous forage or land lying fallow or cultivated with crops under water for a significant part of the year or for a significant part of the crop cycle cover more than 75 % of the arable land. In such cases, the main crop on the remaining arable area shall not cover more than 75 % of that remaining arable land, except where this remaining area is covered by grasses or other herbaceous forage or land lying fallow. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d6ab8ea77807f3eae612af489ba63707] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

#### Article 45 (affected by repeal, see note) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Permanent grassland (affected by repeal, see note) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314][^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-82ed5ab607157b9999be39fd12a1c853][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] Farmers shall not convert or plough permanent grassland situated in areas which, prior to exit day, have been designated by the relevant authority as permanent grasslands which are environmentally sensitive. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] The relevant authority[^c-15f9b7bc68d83b74f64634ac53594c52] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] shall ensure that the ratio of areas of permanent grassland to the total agricultural area declared by the farmers in accordance with point (a) of the first subparagraph of Article 72(1) of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013 does not decrease by more than 5 % compared to *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] the reference ratio[^c-ee15286419c8fdff36e61d3b865ca863] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] established by *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] the relevant authority[^c-649aca186bb238a17eca78ddd96f1f96] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] in 2015 by dividing areas of permanent grassland referred to in point (a) of the second subparagraph of this paragraph by the total agricultural area referred to in point (b) of that subparagraph. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

For the purposes of establishing the reference ratio referred to in the first subparagraph: *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] "areas of permanent grassland" means the land under permanent pasture declared in 2012, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] [^c-144538158026ad8fa37e3d152b21f17a]... in accordance with Regulation (EC) No 73/2009 by the farmers subject to the obligations under this Chapter, as well as the areas of permanent grassland declared in 2015 in accordance with point (a) of the first subparagraph of Article 72(1) of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013 by the farmers subject to the obligations under this Chapter that have not been declared as land under permanent pasture in 2012 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] [^c-8923d790353aeaf1668873371376f434]...; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] "total agricultural area" means the agricultural area declared in 2015 in accordance with point (a) of the first subparagraph of Article 72(1) of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013 by farmers subject to the obligations under this Chapter. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

The reference ratio of permanent grassland shall be recalculated in cases where farmers subject to the obligations under this Chapter have an obligation to reconvert an area into permanent grassland in 2015 or in 2016 in accordance with Article 93 of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013. In such cases, these areas shall be added to the areas of permanent grassland referred to in point (a) of the second subparagraph of this paragraph. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

The ratio of permanent grassland shall be established each year on the basis of the areas declared by the farmers subject to the obligations under this Chapter for that year in accordance with point (a) of the first subparagraph of Article 72(1) of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

The obligation under this paragraph shall apply across the constituent nation.[^c-eb65a46330e388e0530dbe1b629e28d5] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

[^c-ab11a991a867134e842c7b6bd39c0d63]... *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

3 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] Where it is established that the ratio referred to in paragraph 2 has decreased by more than 5 % *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] in the constituent nation, the relevant authority[^c-03664be135857f876f9256aa0aac0d6e] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] shall impose obligations at holding level to reconvert land into permanent grassland for those farmers who have land at their disposal which was converted from land under permanent pasture or from permanent grassland into land for other uses during a period in the past. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

However, where the amount of areas of permanent grassland in absolute terms established in accordance with point (a) of the second subparagraph of paragraph 2 is maintained within certain limits, the obligation set out in the first subparagraph of paragraph 2 shall be considered to have been complied with. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

#### Article 46 (affected by repeal, see note) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Ecological focus area (affected by repeal, see note) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314][^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] Where the arable land of a holding covers more than 15 hectares, the farmer shall ensure that, from 1 January 2015, an area corresponding to at least 5 % of the arable land of the holding that the farmer declared in accordance with point (a) of the first subparagraph of Article 72(1) of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013 and, if they are considered to be ecological focus area by the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] relevant authority[^c-2460d2dd690de746eb9be0285b769d60] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] in accordance with paragraph 2 of this Article, including the areas mentioned in points (c), (d), (g), (h), (k) and (l) of that paragraph is ecological focus area. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

From 1 January 2027 the percentage referred to in the first subparagraph of this paragraph is increased from 5% to 7%. *(Extent: S)*[^c-f66a05b1218a121f7cd3c93a84b9f8ac] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-349d000318a6f64b68a8fc53ee56386a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

### CHAPTER 5 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note) — Payment for young farmers (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

#### Article 50 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). General rules (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^M_E_6ef0b43a-1822-4a41-bbe6-ac1f6c081639][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

*Extent: E+N.I.*

1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] The relevant authority[^c-57ba5d756b1f8d2e18424e93986ae0dd] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] shall grant an annual payment to young farmers who are entitled to a payment under the *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] farm sustainability payment scheme[^c-a4652b248de57151b8b79c4d91d683fd] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] or the single area payment scheme referred to in Chapter 1 ("payment for young farmers"). *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] For the purposes of this Chapter, 'young farmers', means natural persons: *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] who are setting up for the first time an agricultural holding as head of the holding, or who have already set up such a holding during the five years preceding the first submission of an application under the *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] farm sustainability payment scheme *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] or the single area payment scheme referred to in Article 72(1) of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013; and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] who are no more than 40 years of age in the year of submission of the application referred to in point (a). *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

2a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4485eb83ea291fe831a9748fb16abf81][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] A farmer as defined in Article 4(1)(a) who has been rejected for the payment for young farmers in three or more scheme years shall no longer be eligible for the payment for young farmers. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

A young farmer as defined in Article 50(2) who has been rejected for the payment for young farmers, in three or more scheme years shall no longer be eligible for a payment for young farmers. For the purpose of this sub paragraph, a young farmer shall include those who make an application for the payment for young farmers but are rejected due to not meeting the requirements of Article 50(2)(a). *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

This paragraph shall not apply in the 2021 scheme year. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

3 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-1f35a5193f6ae7888c19348a89b371d4][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] Young farmers applying for the payment under this Article must also meet any objective and non-discriminatory requirements specified by the relevant authority prior to exit day regarding appropriate skills and/or training requirements. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

4 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] Without prejudice to the application *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] [^c-6882760409c26daec801b847f191ddb4]... of reduction of payments in accordance with Article 11 *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] [^c-9841cf35b236b18273d2a793579b9512]..., and to the application of Article 63 of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013, the payment for young farmers shall be granted annually upon activation of payment entitlements by the farmer *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] [^c-38249fe7f194e35230f33df091ce2576].... *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

That period of five years shall also apply for farmers who have received payment for young farmers in respect of claims before claim year 2018. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

By way of derogation from the second sentence of the first subparagraph, *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] the relevant authority[^c-d663b003acbf9971e030f4ea8e6dea56] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] may decide that, for those young farmers who set up in accordance with point (a) of paragraph 2 in the period 2010-2013, the five-year period is to be reduced by the number of years which have elapsed between the setting up referred to in point (a) of paragraph 2 and the first submission of the application for the payment for young farmers. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

#### Article 50. General rules[^M_E_e56b66a7-fbf9-476f-d567-607715fbe688]

*Extent: W*

1. The relevant authority[^V_W_d3143e28-b73e-77f2-8495-57153e3c6051] shall grant an annual payment to young farmers who are entitled to a payment under the basic payment scheme or the single area payment scheme referred to in Chapter 1 ("payment for young farmers").

2. For the purposes of this Chapter, 'young farmers', means natural persons:

- (a) who are setting up for the first time an agricultural holding as head of the holding, or who have already set up such a holding during the five years preceding the first submission of an application under the basic payment scheme or the single area payment scheme referred to in Article 72(1) of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013; and
- (b) who are no more than 40 years of age in the year of submission of the application referred to in point (a).

3.[^c-b4cc59eb8993acb576330c510546c8de] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

4. Without prejudice to the application [^c-688daed801abcf66f62c78eefe361df9]... of reduction of payments in accordance with Article 11 and of linear reductions in accordance with Article 7 of this Regulation, and to the application of Article 63 of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013, the payment for young farmers shall be granted annually upon activation of payment entitlements by the farmer [^V_W_21f2e9a3-86fe-4f64-d02c-a615e9a3bb98]....

5.[^V_W_5e01fd3a-01a2-f0a1-395e-143989afb672] The payment for young farmers shall be granted per farmer for a period of five years, starting from the first submission of the application for the payment for young farmers provided that that submission takes place within the five years following the setting up referred to in point (a) of paragraph 2. That period of five years shall also apply for farmers who have received payment for young farmers in respect of claims before claim year 2018.

[^c-71774aeb7a9c7911bbf8007f2d5e9329]...

6. Each year, in accordance with the legislation applying in the constituent nation, the relevant authority[^V_W_0bd24c28-2fe5-ef2f-7a08-25078dc50e2a] shall calculate the amount of the payment for young farmers by multiplying the number of entitlements the farmer has activated in accordance with Article 32(1) by a figure corresponding to:

- (a) between 25 % and 50 % of the average value of the owned or leased-in payment entitlements held by the farmer; or
- (b) between 25 % and 50 % of an amount calculated by dividing a fixed percentage of the relevant authority’s share[^V_W_2b9fd094-2ce0-afa6-a5fd-6c97ad23a5c4] of the national ceiling for the calendar year 2019 [^V_W_2ae12dd1-e5b6-8341-d863-d34be00f6cd5]... by the number of all eligible hectares declared in the constituent nation[^V_W_e44909d0-3340-c797-f0ce-d5833958a074] in 2015 in accordance with Article 33(1). The fixed percentage shall be equal to the percentage of the relevant authority’s share of the national ceiling which remained for the basic payment scheme in the constituent nation for 2015.[^V_W_04f1739e-6069-ecc9-f489-3ddeb096c27f]

7.[^V_W_db90ec10-a82e-7868-a680-43d81241dfff] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

8.[^c-08a3e07e95055b846a20e438e3684d67] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

9. The[^V_W_a7fdf8aa-f20e-d161-872c-5f8e9ba68021] maximum limit applicable to the number of payment entitlements activated by the farmer or to the number of eligible hectares declared by the farmer is the single maximum limit set by the relevant authority prior to exit day[^V_W_9b0ec9a4-08be-3eee-212f-da5da51350ff]. That limit shall not be below 25 or above 90. The relevant authority[^V_W_3aa9eff1-9f32-95d5-a43f-bbd5396b8aa7] shall respect that limit when applying paragraphs 6 and 8[^V_W_f63292c8-1db0-2064-f2fc-af95d6a3ef6c].

10.[^V_W_22662eb2-2164-5ebf-9bd4-4af3bfa62123] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

10A.[^c-5fdbdc4e4f2eec81a8371da25e40c5f7] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

11. In order to guarantee the protection of the rights of beneficiaries and to avoid discrimination among them, the appropriate authority may make regulations[^V_W_d0c35ae8-2f44-03c3-269b-e952ca219a91] concerning the conditions under which a legal person may be considered to be eligible to receive the payment for young farmers.

#### Article 50. General rules[^M_E_51dde63a-cb0d-4542-a1ea-746e3ea6661a]

*Extent: S*

1. The relevant authority[^V_S_eae98f57-5474-a7c3-0bbf-ab60d9cba04f] shall grant an annual payment to young farmers who are entitled to a payment under the basic payment scheme or the single area payment scheme referred to in Chapter 1 ("payment for young farmers").

2. For the purposes of this Chapter, 'young farmers', means natural persons:

- (a) who are setting up for the first time an agricultural holding as head of the holding, or who have already set up such a holding during the five years preceding the first submission of an application under the basic payment scheme or the single area payment scheme referred to in Article 72(1) of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013; and
- (b) who are no more than 40 years of age in the year of submission of the application referred to in point (a).

3.[^V_S_df89c35c-5e09-a718-ab1c-dbc6d441fdeb] Young farmers applying for the payment under this Article must also meet any objective and non-discriminatory requirements specified by the relevant authority prior to exit day regarding appropriate skills and/or training requirements.

4. Without prejudice to the application [^c-cc2886620adb6a8f829172b2cee2615a]... of reduction of payments in accordance with Article 11 [^c-19a77ba012b4b21334936f6f3f51c389]... of this Regulation, and to the application of Article 63 of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013, the payment for young farmers shall be granted annually upon activation of payment entitlements by the farmer [^V_S_539667df-890b-49a6-afc8-962c8ab4337a]....

5.[^V_S_04296d14-bb5d-beb9-a798-e3af1f15649b] The payment for young farmers shall be granted per farmer for a period of five years, starting from the first submission of the application for the payment for young farmers provided that that submission takes place within the five years following the setting up referred to in point (a) of paragraph 2. That period of five years shall also apply for farmers who have received payment for young farmers in respect of claims before claim year 2018.

By way of derogation from the second sentence of the first subparagraph, the relevant authority[^V_S_2629d2d9-a9a3-a45a-2f20-c5f31083ec3f] may decide that, for those young farmers who set up in accordance with point (a) of paragraph 2 in the period 2010-2013, the five-year period is to be reduced by the number of years which have elapsed between the setting up referred to in point (a) of paragraph 2 and the first submission of the application for the payment for young farmers.

6. Each year, in accordance with the legislation applying in the constituent nation, the relevant authority[^V_S_28605006-75ff-9d46-89f6-9d88039986bf] shall calculate the amount of the payment for young farmers by multiplying the number of entitlements the farmer has activated in accordance with Article 32(1) by a figure corresponding to:

- (a) between 25 % and 50 % of the average value of the owned or leased-in payment entitlements held by the farmer; or
- (b) between 25 % and 50 % of an amount calculated by dividing a fixed percentage of the relevant authority’s share[^V_S_7e283544-eea7-2317-726d-3fbd7b1bb8c1] of the national ceiling for the calendar year 2019 [^V_S_b4715a12-b181-925d-237e-866845dd697c]... by the number of all eligible hectares declared in the constituent nation[^V_S_97edb86c-694c-9a16-466a-00eb402482f1] in 2015 in accordance with Article 33(1). The fixed percentage shall be equal to the percentage of the relevant authority’s share of the national ceiling which remained for the basic payment scheme in the constituent nation for 2015.[^V_S_6cc9476c-cd48-9051-61a7-b734894add7f]

7.[^V_S_55c3d0d7-1e23-1599-6a29-e62dc1bba225] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

8. By way of derogation from paragraph 6[^V_S_de83a8a7-002a-03af-83d3-3dbde19c4091] of this Article, where the legislation applying in the constituent nation so provides, the relevant authority shall[^V_S_8c60d1a8-6ece-e538-1293-8e6502473f7b] calculate each year the amount of the payment for young farmers by multiplying a figure corresponding to a value between 25 % and 50 % of the [^V_S_247ff492-b009-66e6-5ad4-7a00ccbcd904]... average payment per hectare in the constituent nation[^V_S_c41072c8-8c64-599f-03d1-579b9bef7190] by the number of entitlements that the farmer has activated in accordance with Article 32(1) [^V_S_da05466d-5809-9bc8-28bf-cd417d292ed4]....

The [^V_S_1b47288c-ba7c-260d-a36e-c05e1f1da35f]... average payment per hectare in the constituent nation[^V_S_145c7622-c3b5-46c3-9b0f-80a1f9c566be] shall be calculated by dividing the relevant authority’s share of[^V_S_4110423e-e2b8-265a-027b-a6c746563154] the national ceiling for the calendar year 2019 [^V_S_0227dfe7-f29c-6a3b-972a-2d4845e0bd7d]... by the number of eligible hectares declared in the constituent nation[^V_S_a4ed209c-ce80-ae93-0268-e4247788d3dd] in 2015 in accordance with Article 33(1) [^V_S_183cac66-7e5a-0575-e439-488cfef6ae5e]....

The[^V_S_abbe3de0-2bea-89e8-3651-da478eb5b75f] maximum limit applicable to the number of payment entitlements activated by the farmer or to the number of eligible hectares declared by the farmer is the single maximum limit set by the relevant authority prior to exit day[^V_S_922848f3-8f72-47ce-baa3-5b35ed39e6f0]. That limit shall not be below 25 or above 90. The relevant authority[^V_S_990e83dd-13c6-6774-f9e0-ca0a112d219e] shall respect that limit when applying paragraphs 6 and 8[^V_S_99d9ebfa-a4b0-d171-2e34-bada15cc6a5d].

#### Article 51 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Financial provisions (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^M_E_16e18034-1dd3-4ac3-ed51-48ef8fb1de50][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

*Extent: E+N.I.*

1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4736045ed0968eba871521e363c3963a][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] In order to finance the payment for young farmers, the relevant authority shall use a percentage, which shall not be higher than 2% of *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] the total[^c-9981aa137e053756045611a7f03e0785] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] ceiling. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] Without prejudice to the maximum of 2 % laid down in paragraph 1 of this Article, where the total amount of the payment for young farmers applied for in a *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] constituent nation in a particular year exceeds the amount calculated in accordance with paragraph 1, and where that amount is lower than 2% of the[^c-be2937885fa09d9a8ab498364fbc0a10] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] total[^c-eeb56762adc044f3bb2ad5c15c262522] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] ceiling, the relevant authority *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] shall finance the difference by applying point (f) of the first subparagraph of Article 30(7) in the relevant year, by applying a linear reduction to all payments to be granted to all farmers in accordance with Article 32 *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] , or by both means[^c-a2e0d3b9f6b6e3568782b45cac128d8c] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314][^c-1ebfded157fb94a28bbe315b7b6e2b76].... *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

#### Article 51. Financial provisions[^M_E_3f1bd844-c1ee-4e6e-ba19-8039a2357bf3]

*Extent: W*

1.[^V_W_9d24b658-b3fc-6311-044f-d332b1265886] In order to finance the payment for young farmers, the relevant authority shall use a percentage, which shall not be higher than 2% of the total[^c-ed06153b0476c6a9bb3fad995fe121f8] ceiling.

Where the relevant authority decided to revise the estimated percentage and this decision was notified to the Commission by 1 August 2019, that revised percentage will have effect for calendar year 2020.

2. Without prejudice to the maximum of 2 % laid down in paragraph 1 of this Article, where the total amount of the payment for young farmers applied for in a constituent nation in a particular year exceeds the amount calculated in accordance with paragraph 1, and where that amount is lower than 2% of the[^V_W_3fae8caf-f971-a88a-ec4f-9f052d25f04c] total[^c-a389365cbd312a6d1d172677f96c66d5] ceiling, the relevant authority shall finance the difference by applying point (f) of the first subparagraph of Article 30(7) in the relevant year, by applying a linear reduction to all payments to be granted to all farmers in accordance with Article 32 , or by both means[^V_W_99303b6d-6854-dc17-7769-50db96ddb678][^V_W_5eca94af-4e84-ff36-71f3-6290eb5113e2]....

3. Where the total amount of the payment for young farmers applied for in a constituent nation[^V_W_ee4fba3a-8ffe-5d2f-da0d-15a41eb6a3f1] in a particular year exceeds the amount calculated in accordance with paragraph 1, and where that amount is equal to 2% of[^V_W_f9d86803-424e-6e99-f00e-306dfef9e425] the total[^c-b4b0d6dea13f6a8d95ae6d1ff1ebb3fd] ceiling, the relevant authority shall apply a linear reduction to the amounts to be paid pursuant to Article 50 in order to comply with that ceiling.

4.[^V_W_c2905e7c-7df1-908e-d62d-fd1603b19d37] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

#### Article 51. Financial provisions[^M_E_42ecd291-ba88-4b7d-bd6a-5ae805d4b1f0]

*Extent: S*

1.[^V_S_befee8e8-b09b-45c2-2ca9-e53a5934d545] In order to finance the payment for young farmers, the relevant authority shall use a percentage, which shall not be higher than 2% of the total ceiling[^c-771b75a94ee64d52592405bf9f38a69e].

Where the relevant authority decides by 1 August in a calendar year to revise the percentage, that revised percentage will have effect for the following calendar year.[^c-9edb78a875ee5e6b761f912d59f5c1f7]

2. Without prejudice to the maximum of 2 % laid down in paragraph 1 of this Article, where the total amount of the payment for young farmers applied for in a constituent nation in a particular year exceeds the amount calculated in accordance with paragraph 1, and where that amount is lower than 2% of the[^V_S_c9280dc5-cece-f87d-872a-61d7bd2c734e] total ceiling[^c-792bdafe6ddca2ff90c0976a5c3345cd], the relevant authority shall finance the difference by applying point (f) of the first subparagraph of Article 30(7) in the relevant year, by applying a linear reduction to all payments to be granted to all farmers in accordance with Article 32 , or by both means[^V_S_77e559b2-f496-2ced-c6ba-a97b5ff1f995][^V_S_2214ab28-4600-4a59-10d6-0b1c4c3d2e7d]....

Where the total amount of the payment for young farmers applied for in a constituent nation[^V_S_eb259210-9107-0de5-b03a-519a820779fd] in a particular year exceeds the amount calculated in accordance with paragraph 1, and where that amount is equal to 2% of[^V_S_8ad5823a-5d5f-07b9-1887-448bad0ec4f6] the total ceiling[^c-c9fecb1cd93db6913f7175d5adfda6ea], the relevant authority shall apply a linear reduction to the amounts to be paid pursuant to Article 50 in order to comply with that ceiling.

## TITLE IV (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note) — COUPLED SUPPORT (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

### CHAPTER 1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note) — Voluntary coupled support (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

#### Article 52 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). General rules (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] The relevant authority[^c-e978db9cd8589cf83bd5001cbced5909] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] may grant coupled support to farmers under the conditions laid down in this Chapter (in this Chapter referred to as "coupled support"). *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] Coupled support may be granted to the following sectors and productions: cereals, oilseeds, protein crops, grain legumes, flax, hemp, rice, nuts, starch potato, milk and milk products, seeds, sheepmeat and goatmeat, beef and veal, olive oil, silkworms, dried fodder, hops, sugar beet, cane and chicory, fruit and vegetables and short rotation coppice. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

3 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] Coupled support may only be granted to those sectors or to those regions of a *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] constituent nation[^c-5f7b6fc618869e014fe2f4913df9d004] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] where specific types of farming or specific agricultural sectors that are particularly important for economic, social or environmental reasons undergo certain difficulties. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

4 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] By way of derogation from paragraph 3, coupled support may also be granted to farmers who: *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] on 31 December 2014, have payment entitlements granted in accordance with Section 2 of Chapter 3 of Title III and Article 71m of Regulation (EC) No 1782/2003 and in accordance with Article 60 and the fourth paragraph of Article 65 of Regulation (EC) No 73/2009; and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] have at their disposal no eligible hectares for the activation of payment entitlements under the *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] basic payment scheme *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-8b946146c4ab503074c180e67438dacb] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]farm sustainability payment scheme *(Extent: N.I.)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] as referred to in Chapter 1 of Title III of this Regulation. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

5 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c000007][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

6 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] Coupled support is a production-limiting scheme that shall take the form of an annual payment based on fixed areas and yields or on a fixed number of animals and shall respect financial ceilings *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] to be *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-80c59ae9a0454a4e925ce833fcdbde1a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] jointly agreed by the relevant authorities for each constituent nation.[^c-4d41ee76cd69f39efa87125cbe0d66bc] *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-80c59ae9a0454a4e925ce833fcdbde1a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]to be determined by the relevant authority. *(Extent: N.I.)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

7 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] In the case of a legal person, or a group of natural or legal persons, *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-5c5e7d21eab5ff04b3a0b20eb64c7eb0] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] the relevant authority[^c-ceedb73c523838e2349d8a3aab2320ba] *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-5c5e7d21eab5ff04b3a0b20eb64c7eb0] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] may apply the limits referred to in paragraph 6 at the level of the members of those legal persons or groups where national law provides for the individual members to assume rights and obligations comparable to those of individual farmers who have the status of a head of holding, in particular as regards their economic, social and tax status, provided that they have contributed to strengthening the agricultural structures of the legal persons or groups concerned. *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-5c5e7d21eab5ff04b3a0b20eb64c7eb0] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

8 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] Any coupled support granted under this Article shall be consistent with *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] measures provided for in Regulation (EU) No 1305/2013 and Regulation (EU) No 1308/2013 and any other measures specified for the purposes of this paragraph by virtue of paragraph 9[^c-d60f5b9b9cb8caf6baab0bebbac337cd] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

9 (Extent: N.I.) (affected by repeal, see note) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-42bf76c46cefa6c3683514e354ed4102][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] In order to ensure efficient and targeted use of *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-42bf76c46cefa6c3683514e354ed4102] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] agricultural support[^c-9ca4e09c65680b430bc69d803101b62b] *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-42bf76c46cefa6c3683514e354ed4102] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] and to avoid double funding under other similar support instruments, the *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-42bf76c46cefa6c3683514e354ed4102] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] Secretary of State may make regulations[^c-cf16df539f709b97ca254bb166e61807] *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-42bf76c46cefa6c3683514e354ed4102] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] laying down: *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-42bf76c46cefa6c3683514e354ed4102] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

(Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] In order to ensure efficient and targeted use of agricultural support and to avoid double funding under other similar support instruments, DAERA may make regulations laying down the conditions for granting coupled support. *(Extent: N.I.)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

10 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] The *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] Secretary of State *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72fb7b7cb5c35f26a5602f9c0a7bf353][^c-7443d941caa793102096b40321b80b1a] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]DAERA *(Extent: N.I.)* *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] may make regulations *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] supplementing this Regulation as regards measures in order to avoid beneficiaries of voluntary coupled support suffering from structural market imbalances in a sector. Those *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] regulations[^c-73716c574f2bacafc2fbd9f6930e2ebc] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] may allow *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] for support to[^c-63dfc1653d25a0e5c46c7dc1e3bd2ec8] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] continue to be paid until 2020 on the basis of the production units for which voluntary coupled support was granted in a past reference period. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

11 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-ac56bf53a59054da9a06e01f4eac097a][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] The Secretary of State may not make regulations under paragraph 9 or 10 without the consent of each of the relevant authorities for Wales, Scotland and Northern Ireland. *(Extent: N.I.)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-8192a8123f2b0b1f8f0a7d631f331d36] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

#### Article 68 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Processing and protection of personal data (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] The relevant authority may collect personal data, in relation to the constituent nation, for the purposes of checking, controlling, monitoring, evaluating and auditing direct payments or for the purpose of complying with requirements laid down in international agreements.[^c-c2f0b38c8ed0fc47b917e9cb603e1727] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] They shall not process this data in a way that is incompatible with those purposes. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] Where personal data are processed for monitoring and evaluation purposes as referred to in *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] paragraph 1[^c-89547066f16119b1b7b1f4664118f322] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314], they shall be made anonymous and processed in aggregated form only. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

3 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] Personal data shall be processed in accordance with *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] Regulation (EU) 2016/679 and the Data Protection Act 2018[^c-a7a0cbedb6fef37b6f95ed6993d90657] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]. In particular, such data shall not be stored in a form which permits identification of data subjects for longer than is necessary for the purposes for which they were collected or for which they are further processed, taking into account *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] any statutory minimum retention periods[^c-ea276551b2ee0d66bb76bf7af0125133] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

4 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] The relevant authority[^c-13ac3ca3816008912ac5e46c86fcf352] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] shall inform the data subjects that their personal data may be processed by *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] UK bodies and bodies in the constituent nation[^c-f7f863c9914209e30f199e4adae70552] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] in accordance with paragraph 1, and that in this respect they enjoy the rights set out in *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] Regulation (EU) 2016/679 and the Data Protection Act 2018[^c-2a4c5a04fdd565a708ad5584fc952d96] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314], respectively. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

5 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] This Article shall be subject to Articles 111 to 114 of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

#### Article 72 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Repeals (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] Regulation (EC) No 637/2008 is repealed with effect from 1 January 2014. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

However, it shall continue to apply until 31 December 2017 in respect of Member States which have exercised the option laid down in the second subparagraph of Article 4(1) of that Regulation. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] Regulation (EC) No 73/2009 is repealed. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

However, it shall continue to apply in respect of aid applications relating to claim years starting before 1 January 2015.[^c000006] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

Without prejudice to paragraph 3, references to the repealed Regulation shall be construed as references to this Regulation or Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013 and shall be read in accordance with the correlation table set out in Annex XI to this Regulation. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

3 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] The references made in this Regulation to Regulations (EC) No 73/2009 and (EC) No 1782/2003 shall be understood as being made to those Regulations such as they were in force before their repeal. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
