## ANNEX IX (affected by repeal, see note) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note) — List of equivalent practices referred to in Article 43(3) (affected by repeal, see note) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-a5ca92d8b7e95df6c59cffe92e4ff29f][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314][^c-a5ca92d8b7e95df6c59cffe92e4ff29f][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

- I.. Practices equivalent to crop diversification: *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a5ca92d8b7e95df6c59cffe92e4ff29f] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
  - ((1)) [^c-5bc706c84490e0993645b995ce4c8aa0]Crop diversification *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a5ca92d8b7e95df6c59cffe92e4ff29f] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

    Requirement: at least three crops, the main crop covering a maximum of 75 %, and any one or more of the following applying: *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a5ca92d8b7e95df6c59cffe92e4ff29f] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

    - there are at least four crops, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a5ca92d8b7e95df6c59cffe92e4ff29f] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
    - lower maximum thresholds apply, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a5ca92d8b7e95df6c59cffe92e4ff29f] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
    - there is a more appropriate selection of crops, such as, for example, leguminous, protein crops, crops not requiring irrigation or pesticide treatments, as appropriate, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a5ca92d8b7e95df6c59cffe92e4ff29f] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
    - regional varieties of old, traditional or endangered crop types are included on at least 5 % of the rotated area. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a5ca92d8b7e95df6c59cffe92e4ff29f] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
  - ((2)) Crop rotation *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a5ca92d8b7e95df6c59cffe92e4ff29f] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

    Requirement: at least three crops, the main crop covering a maximum of 75 %, and any one or both of the following applying: *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a5ca92d8b7e95df6c59cffe92e4ff29f] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

    - a more environmentally beneficial multiannual sequence of crops and/or fallow is followed, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a5ca92d8b7e95df6c59cffe92e4ff29f] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
    - there are at least four crops. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a5ca92d8b7e95df6c59cffe92e4ff29f] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
  - ((3)) Winter soil cover *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a5ca92d8b7e95df6c59cffe92e4ff29f] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314][^f00025]
  - ((4)) Catch crops *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a5ca92d8b7e95df6c59cffe92e4ff29f] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314][^f00025]
- II.. Practices equivalent to maintenance of permanent grassland: *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a5ca92d8b7e95df6c59cffe92e4ff29f] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
  - ((1)) Management of meadows or pastures *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a5ca92d8b7e95df6c59cffe92e4ff29f] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

    Requirement: maintenance of permanent grassland and any one or more of the following: *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a5ca92d8b7e95df6c59cffe92e4ff29f] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

    - Cutting regime or appropriate mowing (dates, methods, limits) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a5ca92d8b7e95df6c59cffe92e4ff29f] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
    - Maintenance of landscape features on permanent grassland and control of scrub *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a5ca92d8b7e95df6c59cffe92e4ff29f] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
    - Specified grass varieties and/or seeding regime for renewal depending on the grassland type, with no destruction of high nature value *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a5ca92d8b7e95df6c59cffe92e4ff29f] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
    - Evacuation of forage or hay *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a5ca92d8b7e95df6c59cffe92e4ff29f] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
    - Appropriate management for steep slopes *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a5ca92d8b7e95df6c59cffe92e4ff29f] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
    - Fertiliser regime *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a5ca92d8b7e95df6c59cffe92e4ff29f] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
    - Pesticide restrictions *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a5ca92d8b7e95df6c59cffe92e4ff29f] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
  - ((2)) Extensive grazing systems *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a5ca92d8b7e95df6c59cffe92e4ff29f] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

    Requirement: maintenance of permanent grassland and any one or more of the following: *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a5ca92d8b7e95df6c59cffe92e4ff29f] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

    - Extensive grazing (timing, maximum stocking density) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a5ca92d8b7e95df6c59cffe92e4ff29f] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
    - Shepherding or mountain pastoralism *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a5ca92d8b7e95df6c59cffe92e4ff29f] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
    - Using local or traditional breeds for grazing the permanent grassland *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a5ca92d8b7e95df6c59cffe92e4ff29f] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
- III.. Practices equivalent with ecological focus area: *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a5ca92d8b7e95df6c59cffe92e4ff29f] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

  Requirement: application of any of the following practices on at least the percentage of the arable land set pursuant to Article 46(1): *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a5ca92d8b7e95df6c59cffe92e4ff29f] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

  - ((1)) Ecological set-aside *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a5ca92d8b7e95df6c59cffe92e4ff29f] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
  - ((2)) Creation of "buffer zones" for high nature value areas, Natura 2000 or other biodiversity protection sites, including along hedgerows and water courses *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a5ca92d8b7e95df6c59cffe92e4ff29f] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
  - ((3)) Management of uncultivated buffer strips and field margins (cutting regime, local or specified grass varieties and/or seeding regime, re-seeding with regional varieties, no use of pesticides, no disposal of manure and/or mineral fertilisers, no irrigation, no soil sealing) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a5ca92d8b7e95df6c59cffe92e4ff29f] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
  - ((4)) Borders, in-field strips and patches managed for wildlife or specific fauna (herbaceous border, protection of nests, wildflower strips, local seed mix, unharvested crops) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a5ca92d8b7e95df6c59cffe92e4ff29f] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
  - ((5)) Management (pruning, trimming, dates, methods, restoration) of landscape features (trees, hedgerows, riparian woody vegetation, stone walls (terraces), ditches, ponds) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a5ca92d8b7e95df6c59cffe92e4ff29f] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
  - ((6)) Keeping arable peaty or wet soils under grass (with no use of fertilisers and no use of plant protection products) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a5ca92d8b7e95df6c59cffe92e4ff29f] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
  - ((7)) Production on arable land with no use of fertiliser (mineral fertiliser and manure) and/or plant protection products, and not irrigated, not sown with the same crop two years in a row and on a fixed place *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a5ca92d8b7e95df6c59cffe92e4ff29f] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314][^f00025]
  - ((8)) Conversion of arable land into permanent grassland extensively used *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a5ca92d8b7e95df6c59cffe92e4ff29f] *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

## ANNEX IXa (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note) — Matters to be included in a plan or report referred in Article 43a(1) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-720db5ca08b1ba08f0dfbbf98c99d901][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

*Extent: S*

### 1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Animal health and welfare plan (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

(1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] An animal health and welfare plan (in this paragraph, a “plan”) must have been prepared not more than one year before the start of the claim year to which it relates. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

(2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] A plan must include information about animals kept on the holding, including in particular the number and sex of each species of animal. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

(3 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] A plan must include the name and address of, and contact information for, each person who manages animals on the holding. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

(4 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] A plan must include— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] the name and address of, and contact information for, any attending veterinary surgeon; and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] information about the veterinary services expected to be provided in respect of animals on the holding. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

(5 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] A plan must include information about animals kept on the holding for breeding, including in particular— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

- (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] the number and species of such animals; and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
- (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] the number and species of animals expected to be born on the holding during the claim year. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

(6 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] A plan must include information about animal management activities expected to be carried out during the claim year, including in particular— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] the type of activity (for example, scanning a pregnant animal); and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] the period during which the activity is expected to be carried out. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

(7 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] A plan must include information about the method by which an animal would where necessary or appropriate be euthanised, including in particular— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] the killing method; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] the name of, and contact information for, the person who will kill the animal; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] the qualification or training which enables that person to euthanise the animal. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

(8 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] A plan must include information about how fallen stock is managed. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

(9 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] A plan must include information about how animal disease is managed, and in particular must specify for each disease that may in the opinion of the person who manages an animal (acting reasonably) occur on the holding— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] the likelihood of the disease occurring; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] the risks to animal health of the disease; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] the measures that are considered necessary or appropriate— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] to prevent the onset or spread of a disease; and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] to prevent or mitigate harm from a disease. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

(10 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] In this paragraph— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] a reference to an animal means an alpaca, an animal of a bovine species, a goat, a llama, a pig, or a sheep; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] a reference to a disease includes a parasite; and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] a reference to a person who manages an animal includes, but is not limited to, the keeper. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

### 2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Carbon plan (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

(1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] A carbon plan (in this paragraph, a “plan”) must have been prepared not more than five years before the start of the claim year to which it relates. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

(2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] A plan must include information about emissions from the holding over at least a 12 month period, measured in tonnes of carbon dioxide equivalent. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

(3 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] A plan must include information about how those emissions will be prevented or reduced. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

(4 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] In this paragraph— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] a reference to emissions means emissions of a greenhouse gas— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] from buildings and machinery used for agricultural purposes on the holding; and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] from agricultural activities carried out on the holding; and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] “ *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]**greenhouse gas *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]**” means carbon dioxide, methane and nitrous oxide. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

### 3 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Habitats report (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

(1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] A habitats report (in this paragraph, a “report”) must have been prepared not more than five years before the start of the claim year to which it relates. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

(2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] A report must include a map of land on the holding that is managed on a permanent basis showing, at a minimum, the location of any habitat types described in subparagraph (3). *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

(3 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] The habitat types referred to in subparagraph (2) are— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] coastal land; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] cropped land; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] grassland; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
- (d (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] linear features; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
- (e (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] peatland; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
- (f (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] upland grazings; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
- (g (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] wetlands; and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
- (h (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] land designated by a competent authority for cultural, environmental, historic, scenic or scientific purposes. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

(4 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] In this paragraph— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] “ *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]**linear feature *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]**” includes drystone dykes, hedges, and watercourses; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] a reference to a competent authority is a reference to a body or person exercising a function under an enactment; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] a reference to land managed on a permanent basis does not include land let for a period of less than a year. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

### 4 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Integrated pest management plan (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314][^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

(1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] An integrated pest management plan (in this paragraph, a “plan”) must have been prepared not more than one year before the start of the claim year to which it relates. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

(2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] A plan must describe the measures intended to prevent or control the introduction or spread of plant pests on the holding, including any use of a plant protection product. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

(3 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] A plan must describe how those measures are consistent with the general principles of integrated pest management as described in Annex III of the Directive. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

(4 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] In this paragraph— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] “ *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]**the Directive *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]**” means *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] [Directive 2009/128/EC *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/european/directive/2009/0128)[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] of the European Parliament and of the Council establishing a framework for Community action to achieve the sustainable use of pesticides *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314], as that Directive is defined in regulation 2(1) of the Plant Protection Products (Sustainable Use) Regulations 2012 *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]; and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] “ *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]**plant protection product *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]**” includes herbicides, nematicides, pesticides, plant growth regulators, and slug control agents. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

(1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] A soil report (in this paragraph, a “report”) must have been prepared not more than five years before the start of the claim year to which it relates. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

(2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] A report must provide the specified information for each field on the holding that is— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] region 1 land managed on a permanent basis; and *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] land to which an artificial fertiliser or organic manure is applied. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

(3 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] The specified information is any two of— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] the amount of organic matter in the soil; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] the amount of phosphorus in the soil; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
- (d (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] the amount of potassium in the soil. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

(4 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] In this paragraph— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] “ *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]**region 1 *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]**” means the region applied by the Scottish Ministers under and in accordance with Article 23(1) as region 1 of Scotland and comprising better quality agricultural land; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314] a reference to land managed on a permanent basis does not include land— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4504391a8a07f399f57048325b4f1314]
